# Persona Study 2026-09 — Stage 4: Technical implementation plan

**Status: final (rev 2)**, built on the final n/10 synthesis
([`01-research-synthesis.md`](./01-research-synthesis.md), 54 CIs, bugs B-01…B-53), strategy rev 3
([`02-business-strategy.md`](./02-business-strategy.md)) and the final UX spec
([`03-ux-design-spec.md`](./03-ux-design-spec.md), changelog in its Appendix B). What changed since rev 1 is in the
[changelog](#10-changelog). **Task IDs are stable:** `T-xx.n` belongs to `UX-xx` (= `B-xx`), `T-00.n` are wave-0
foundations (stage 3's shared patterns and contracts), and `T-BUG-nn` carries the number of **bug B-nn** in stage 1
§7. Defects found only while reading the source are `T-BUG-Xn` (renumbered in rev 2 because rev 1's `T-BUG-40…45`
now collide with stage-1 bugs B-40…B-45).

Role: staff engineer. Date: 2026-09-27. Build read: `master` @ `f8f7f74`. Every path below was checked against that
commit; files that do not exist yet are marked **NEW**. Line numbers are indicative (they drift as lanes land).

> **Caveat (carried from stages 1–3).** The users were synthetic (an LLM method-acting each persona on the real
> app). This plan turns strong hypotheses into code; it does not make them true. Where a task rests on a load-bearing
> assumption, its **Validate** note names the real-user test (stage 2 §8.1, V1–V9). Engineering acceptance (tests
> pass) is not product acceptance (users behave as predicted) — the second is measured after T-12 ships analytics.

**Conventions.** Paths are repo-relative. Mobile paths under `apps/mobile/` are written in full the first time in a
section and may be shortened to `app/…` / `src/…` after that. "Kit" = `packages/ui-mobile`. Zod sketches are
indicative: the implementing agent owns exact bounds but must keep every field **optional or defaulted** where the
sketch says so (backward compatibility). Effort: **S** ≈ 1–2 agent-days, **M** ≈ 3–5, **L** ≈ 6–10. "OTA" = ships as
an EAS Update to existing binaries; "Native" = changes the runtime fingerprint and needs `pnpm mobile:release:*`
(and later a store build).

## 0. Summary

**Scope (rev 2, final).** 20 Now items plus stage 3's shared patterns → **147 Now tasks** (`T-00.1`–`T-39.6`)
plus **20 standalone bug tasks**:

- Every one of stage 1's 53 bugs has a `T-BUG` row. 33 of those rows point to the Now task that fixes the bug.
- 7 more defects were found only in the source (`T-BUG-X1`–`X7`).
- The 10 Next items are planned roughly (§5.16), and the Later items get one line each.

**Waves (≈ 6–7 weeks).**

- **Wave 0 (4–5 days):**
  - kit patterns, including the PAT-14 change notice;
  - shared contracts, **all** schema, the new router stubs, nav, flags and the client API-level header;
  - five hotfixes that ship first:
    - the Replace/"what can I make" safety filter (B-34/B-46);
    - the **"next week shown as this week" data bug** (B-13);
    - the calorie ring hidden for users without a goal who don't track (B-31 interim);
    - the budget field that drops input (B-10);
    - the chat guardrail.
- **Wave 1 (≈ 2 weeks), 6 lanes:** safety, gym + chaotic week, plan, entry/legal, **tracking (new)**, data/privacy.
- **Wave 2 (≈ 1.5 weeks), 4 lanes:** home by job, paywall, safety on plan surfaces, gym continued.
- **Wave 3 (≈ 1 week), 2 lanes:** food follows training + share, health consent.
- **Wave 4:** integrate and verify, plus the native release.

Each wave merges to `master`: the API deploys first, then the OTA publishes automatically.

**Schema (all additive; production applies it with `prisma db push`):**

- `DietaryPreferences`: plan shape, safety review, stricter-labels switch.
- **New tables:** `SafetyReport`, `TargetChange`, `ConsentEvent`.
- `ChefProfile`: jobs (incl. `TRACK`), training weekdays + kinds, time zone, own targets + snapshot. `Goal` gains
  `RECOMP` and `PERFORMANCE`.
- `User`: health-consent cache.
- `GymProfile`: vest, quiet-days nudge, session length, per-day reminders, carry-over.
- `ChefReview`: proposals.
- `AiCallLog`: provider, saved, and the enum value `CURATED_PLAN`.
- Defaults flipped to off **for new rows only**: the two email digests and the Sunday auto-plan.

**Native rebuild: one, in wave 4, containing `expo-sharing`** (plus `expo-file-system` pinned as a direct
dependency). It is needed only for sharing the data export (and the gym CSV) as a **file on Android**. Everything
else ships over OTA:

- UX-12 analytics uses a pure-JS PostHog transport, which differs from stage 3's SDK plan (§2.14).
- `ExpoFileSystem` is already in the binary.
- Notifications, the photo picker and the WebView are already present.

**Top risks.**

1. **Allergen liability.** The one filter must now also cover hidden gluten and label-dependent products. The
   regression suite gates CI.
2. **Installed binaries.**
   - Health consent and the Terms checkbox only become required through the `x-chefer-api-level` header, with a
     staged `HEALTH_CONSENT_ENFORCE` rollout.
   - The new goals are mapped to `MAINTAIN` for old clients.
   - Rejecting unsafe Replace picks will surface errors on old builds; this is intended.
3. **Gym engine parity (phone and server)** across the progression fix and carry-over. There are no new reason
   codes.
4. **Targets.** The detection-on-read change log must not become noisy, and the weekly review stops auto-applying
   its calorie dial.
5. **Hot files.** `meal-plan.service.ts`, `preferences.*` and `tracker.*` are single-owner per wave (§7.3).

**Top open questions (§9):**

- ⚖ D-2 flags: own targets are recommended **on**.
- Counsel on existing users' health data and on the analytics default.
- The "curated plans are unlimited" copy is false today (3 a day on free).
- Import counting (previews are the AI cost).
- The coach no longer auto-adjusting.
- Whether a gesture handler joins the native release.

## 1. Architecture constraints (restated)

These are not new rules; they are the repo's rules (`CLAUDE.md`, `apps/mobile/AGENTS.md`, `infrastructure.md`,
`mobile_native_plan.md` §4–§5) applied to this plan. A task that breaks one is not done.

| #   | Constraint                                           | What it means for this plan                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| C1  | **Layering: Router → Service → Repository → Prisma** | Routers in `apps/api/src/routers/` only parse input and call a service. The one safety filter (T-01.2) is an application service; its data access goes through repository interfaces (`I…Repository`). No Prisma in routers or services.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| C2  | **tRPC only** (no REST)                              | Every new capability is a procedure. Chat streaming stays on its existing transport; new chat fields ride on the existing final event (additive).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| C3  | **Zod everywhere**                                   | Every new input has a Zod schema in `@chefer/types` (shared with both clients); every new env var goes through `apps/api/src/lib/env.ts` (no `process.env` elsewhere).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| C4  | **Shared-first**                                     | Taxonomies, recognisers, copy that both platforms render, and every pure rule (landing, card order, week default, price band, plan shape, share format, nudge cap) live in `@chefer/types` / `@chefer/utils` with unit tests. Apps hold UI only.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| C5  | **Additive, backward-compatible API only**           | Production binaries are installed (owner's iPhone 17 and Pixel 8 Pro, M4-4) and each carries an embedded bundle that keeps calling the old contract until it takes an OTA. Rules used below: (a) new procedures, never renamed or removed ones; (b) new **input** fields are optional with a default that reproduces today's behaviour; (c) new **output** fields are added, never retyped or removed; (d) validation is never tightened on an existing procedure for existing clients (the health-consent gate, T-26.3, is the one place this bites — see §2.8); (e) stored value formats do not change meaning (safety arrays stay `string[]` of labels). |
| C6  | **OTA vs native**                                    | `runtimeVersion.policy: 'fingerprint'` (`apps/mobile/app.config.js`). JS-only work ships over EAS Update (auto-published by `deploy.yml` → `mobile-update` after a verified deploy). Any new native module (PostHog RN's device/storage deps, a date-time picker, clipboard, …) changes the fingerprint → `pnpm mobile:release:ios                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | android` and, once in stores, a store release. **An OTA must never be published before the API it calls is deployed** (infrastructure.md §11). |
| C7  | **Platform parity**                                  | Every user-facing change ships on web in the same PR group, or gets a `mobile_parity_backlog.md` row (including **reverse** rows, mobile → web, for gym/landing features web does not have yet).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| C8  | **Docs in the same PR**                              | The CLAUDE.md table: routes → `infrastructure.md` §4 + `business_flow.md`; procedures → §8 + `business_flow.md`; schema → §6; services/repositories → §7; env → §10 + `.env.example`; auth → §9 + `business_flow.md` §4. §8 of this document lists them per lane.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| C9  | **Mobile specifics**                                 | Expo SDK 57 — read the versioned docs before writing code (`apps/mobile/AGENTS.md`); `@chefer/ui` and `@chefer/database` are lint-forbidden in mobile; extensionless imports; Metro on :8083; `export LANG=en_US.UTF-8` before any native build; `ios/` and `android/` are generated, never edited.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| C10 | **No health data in analytics**                      | Event properties are numbers, booleans and string-literal enums only (enforced by type, T-12.1).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

**Facts about the build that shape every task** (verified at `f8f7f74`; they correct a few premises in stage 3):

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Where                                                                   | Consequence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Production applies the schema with `prisma db push --skip-generate` (one-shot `migrate` service), not `migrate deploy`; `prisma/migrations/` holds a single baseline                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `docker-compose.deploy.yml` L32–46                                      | **Only additive schema changes deploy** (new tables, nullable columns, columns with defaults, new enum values). A rename or drop makes `db push` refuse and fails the deploy. Data backfills are idempotent code (the `backfillLegacyServingSizes()`-at-boot pattern), never SQL.                                                             |
| Safety lists live on `DietaryPreferences` (`allergies`, `dietaryRestrictions`, `dislikedIngredients`, `mealsPerDay`, legacy `servingSize`), not `ChefProfile`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `schema.prisma` L236–254                                                | Plan-shape and safety-confirmation fields go on `DietaryPreferences`; jobs and display toggles on `ChefProfile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Recipe detail is `mealPlan.getRecipe`, not `recipe.byId`; picker rows come from `recipe.list` (`All` = recipes that appeared in the user's plans only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `meal-plan.router.ts` L72–77; `favourite-recipe.repository.ts` L186–212 | `safetyChecks` is added to `mealPlan.getRecipe`, plan reads, `recipe.list`, `recipe.discover` and `dashboard.summary`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Chat is an Express text stream (`POST /api/chat`), no Zod, no final event                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `chat.router.ts` L41–106                                                | New chat fields travel as optional body keys and response **headers** (like `X-Chat-Quota-Exhausted`), never a changed stream format.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| No feature-flag mechanism, no server analytics, no mobile analytics                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | env.ts; grep                                                            | Flags (§2.9) and analytics (T-12) are new infrastructure, kept minimal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Tier is `planTier` + `role` (`entitlements.ts` L11–34); gates are `premiumProcedure` or `hasFeature` inside handlers; tier moves are edits to `packages/types/src/plan-features.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | —                                                                       | ⚖ tier decisions (D-2, D-6, D-7) are data edits plus a flag, not new middleware.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Gym weekdays are `RoutineDay.plannedWeekday`; `GymProfile` has no weekdays, no pause column, no set types (only `isWarmup`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `schema.prisma` L706–765; `engine.ts` L61–67                            | "Training days" = active routine weekdays (already what `TrainingNutritionService.trainingSchedule` reads). The progression fix cannot rely on a back-off set type.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `REASON_CODES` is a `z.enum` inside `suggestionSchema`, which is embedded in `sessionDocSchema.prescription`; installed apps park locally-invalid outbox docs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `schemas.ts` L39–75; infrastructure.md §4.3                             | **No new `ReasonCode` values.** New progression situations reuse existing codes and put new facts in `Suggestion.inputs` (a free record).                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| The engine runs on the phone (`applyFinishedSession`) and on the server (`foldHistory`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `session.ts` L374–397                                                   | Progression changes ship API first, then OTA; the server fold is authoritative on the next `gym.bootstrap`. `ENGINE_VERSION` bump busts the persisted query cache.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Native modules in the installed binaries (`apps/mobile/package.json`, `ios/Podfile.lock`): expo-notifications, expo-image-picker, expo-sqlite, expo-secure-store, expo-haptics, expo-image, expo-keep-awake, react-native-webview, react-native-svg, reanimated, NetInfo, **and `ExpoFileSystem` (57.0.6, linked transitively through `expo`)**. **Not** present: expo-sharing, expo-clipboard, a date/time picker, expo-localization, expo-tracking-transparency, any PostHog native module | —                                                                       | Local notifications (UX-36 quiet nudge), the photo picker (UX-21.16), `Linking.openSettings()`, the in-app legal WebView (UX-39) and writing a file all ship over OTA. **Sharing a file** is the one Now capability that is missing: React Native's `Share.share` shares a file `url` on iOS only; on Android it shares text only. See §2.14. |
| All nutrition targets resolve in one function, `resolveDailyTargets` (`application/preferences/preferences.service.ts` L177–222), which already applies the coach dial `ChefProfile.targetAdjustmentKcal` (written by `coach.service.ts` L210) and a 1,200 kcal floor                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | —                                                                       | The own-target override (UX-35) and the "never change silently" log (UX-11) both hook here, once.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

## 2. Cross-cutting technical design

Decisions that several lanes build on. Each is owned by one wave-0 or wave-1 task (named in brackets) and consumed by
the others.

### 2.1 One safety filter (T-01.1–T-01.4)

**Today.** The matcher is `apps/api/src/lib/curated-recipes/safety.ts` (`isRecipeSafe` L223, `findSafetyIssues`
L256, `filterSafeRecipes` L269). It is applied to curated generation (`safeCuratedPools`, `curated-recipes/index.ts`
L86), Discover (`recipe.service.ts` L158), the AI plan post-check (`meal-plan.service.ts` `enforcePlanSafety`
L778–791), the AI swap result (L1053), `allergenWarnings` (L1602) and import previews. It is **not** applied to:
`replaceRecipe` (L1151–1183), `recipe.list` (the Replace picker), `PantryService.whatCanIMake` (`pantry.service.ts`
L188–233, bug B-05), chat context (`chat.service.ts` L68–73, owner only), rebalance (`meal-plan/rebalance.ts`
L222–270, owner only) and `importSave` with `variant: 'original'`. Member dislikes are dropped
(`household.service.ts` L59–72: "dislikes are owner-only").

**Target.** One application service, **NEW** `apps/api/src/application/safety/safety.service.ts`:

```ts
// packages/types/src/safety.ts (NEW) — shared shapes
export const safetyCheckedItemSchema = z.object({ label: z.string(), who: z.string() }); // who = member name | 'you'
export const safetyChecksSchema = z.object({
  checked: z.array(safetyCheckedItemSchema),   // rules this recipe was run through and passed
  conflicts: z.array(z.string()),              // canonical labels it fails (allergy or diet)
  unchecked: z.array(z.string()),              // kept notes the matcher cannot check
});
export const tableSafetySchema = z.object({
  people: z.array(z.object({ who: z.string(), isOwner: z.boolean(),
    items: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), label: z.string(), kind: z.enum(['allergy','diet','dislike']) })),
    notes: z.array(z.string()) })),
  hasRules: z.boolean(),
  needsReview: z.boolean(),                    // legacy free text not yet confirmed (UX-01 b)
});

// SafetyService (apps/api/src/application/safety/safety.service.ts)
loadTable(userId): Promise<TableSafety>        // owner + members, recognised via the taxonomy, + hidden (reported) recipe ids
check(recipe: RecipeData, table): SafetyChecks
filter<T extends RecipeData>(pool: T[], table, opts?: { dislikes: 'hide' | 'mark' }): T[]
```

- The matcher stays one module; its pattern tables become **taxonomy-driven**: each allergen/diet/dislike-category id
  in **NEW** `packages/types/src/safety-taxonomy.ts` names its pattern set (`TREE_NUT_PATTERNS` gains the
  may-contain list: granola, muesli, pesto, praline, marzipan, nut butter, nut milk — bug B-02; dislike categories
  such as `fish` expand to `FISH_PATTERNS` — bug B-04; `vegetarian-no-eggs` and the `egg-free` modifier are new
  `RESTRICTION_RULES` keys — bug B-03). Patterns stay server-side (one enforcement point); the taxonomy carries ids,
  labels, synonyms, `readBack` and `mayContain` copy for the clients.
- **Interpretation, not rewriting (C5e).** Stored arrays stay `string[]` of whatever the client wrote. For every
  stored term the matcher applies **both** the literal match (today's behaviour) **and** the recognised mapping
  (`recogniseSafetyTerm`, shared in `@chefer/utils`). This makes the "over-block until confirmed" rule of UX-01 (b)
  permanent and free: old binaries that keep sending free text are protected by the mapping; nothing a user typed is
  ever silently dropped. New clients write canonical labels (`Tree nuts`), which match literally _and_ via the id.
- **Dislikes become table-wide and hard** in plan generation and the default Replace list (UX-01 AC4), soft
  (`DislikeChip`) in search results. This deliberately changes `mergeHouseholdSafety`; the household test at
  `household.service.test.ts` L131 is updated, not deleted.
- **Every surface calls `SafetyService`:** `MealPlanService` (`loadMergedSafety` L812 delegates), `RecipeService`
  (`list`, `discover`), `PantryService.whatCanIMake`, `ChatService` context and tools, `rebalance.ts`,
  `RecipeImportService` (preview **and** `importSave` of either variant — saving a conflicting recipe stays allowed
  but it is never auto-placed), `ShoppingListService` (custom-line `Check label` chip) and the refuel snacks (§2.6).
- **Reported recipes** (UX-01 d) are part of the table: `SafetyService.loadTable` includes the user's reported
  recipe ids and `filter` removes them.
- **Regression suite** (**NEW** `apps/api/src/application/safety/safety.regression.test.ts`): every curated recipe ×
  a fixture matrix of profiles (each allergen, each diet, each dislike category, mixed households, legacy free-text
  variants such as `nuts`, `no eggs`, `green vegetables`). Runs in CI on every PR; its JSON report is the B-26
  evidence artefact.
- **Rev 2 additions (bugs B-46, B-47):**
  - **Hidden gluten.** "Always-gluten" ingredients (spelt, seitan, semolina, bulgur, couscous, farro, malt, barley, rye,
    regular oats) join `GLUTEN_PATTERNS` (`safety.ts` L71–94). A second, taxonomy-driven list of **label-dependent**
    ingredients (stock/bouillon, curry powder, soy sauce, baking powder, oats, chocolate, sausages) does **not**
    exclude. `check()` returns them as `labelCaveats: { ingredient, rule }[]`, and they are shown as `LabelCaveat` /
    `Buy certified gluten-free`. A new preference, `DietaryPreferences.excludeLabelDependent` (default false), turns
    caveats into exclusions (`Leave out recipes that need a certified gluten-free product`).
  - **Derived diet tags.** `deriveDietTags(recipe)` (the same matcher run against each diet rule, in
    `lib/curated-recipes/safety.ts`) replaces the hard-coded tags wherever a tag is **shown** or **used as `anyTag`**
    evidence. The curated pool's static `dietaryTags` (e.g. Vegetable Paella, `extra-pool.ts` L740) are no longer
    trusted for `gluten-free`, `vegan`, `vegetarian` or `dairy-free`. `RecipeDto` gains `derivedTags?` and
    `tagQualifiers?` (`gluten-free with GF-labelled stock`). Stored `Recipe.dietaryTags` are untouched, so old
    clients still read them. User-entered tags only **add** restrictions.
  - **Replace is served by the filter.** The picker's rows come from `recipe.list({ forTable: true })`, with the
    `FilteredForLine` counts in `hiddenCount` + `filteredFor: string[]`. `replaceRecipe` **rejects** an unsafe
    recipe id (`BAD_REQUEST`, `cause: 'UNSAFE_FOR_TABLE'`, message names the ingredient), unless the new optional
    `acknowledgeConflict: true` is sent (the UX-01 "Add anyway" confirm; only own recipes can be confirmed).
    **Compat:** old binaries never send the flag, so they get the error for unsafe picks. That is intended
    (safety over compatibility), and the message is plain text their error UI already renders.
  - **Filtered lists say they are filtered.** `recipe.discover`, `recipe.list({ forTable })` and chat `whatCanIMake`
    return (or, for chat, state in text) the hidden count. Search results for hidden recipes come back in
    `alsoMatches[]` with `conflicts` so the client can render the collapsed group.

### 2.2 The `safetyChecks` payload (T-02.1)

Additive fields, all optional so old clients ignore them and new clients render nothing when absent (UX-02 edge
case "older API"):

| Procedure                                                                | New field                                                                                                      | Notes                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `mealPlan.getActive` / `getForWeek` / `getById` / `generate` / `restore` | `days[].meals[].recipe.safetyChecks?`, top-level `tableSafety?`                                                | computed in `assemblePlanDto` (L853) next to `withAllergenWarnings` (kept for old clients) |
| `mealPlan.getRecipe`                                                     | `safetyChecks?`                                                                                                | recipe detail + cook mode                                                                  |
| `mealPlan.replaceRecipe`, `mealPlan.swapRecipe`                          | `allergenWarnings?`, `safetyChecks?` on the returned recipe                                                    | today they return neither                                                                  |
| `recipe.list`, `recipe.discover`                                         | `safetyChecks?` per row; `recipe.list` gains `hiddenCount?` when called with the new optional `forTable: true` | picker footer "{n} recipes hidden"                                                         |
| `dashboard.summary`                                                      | `nextMeal.recipe.safetyChecks?`, `tonight…`, `tableSafety?`                                                    | home cards                                                                                 |
| `shoppingList.getForWeek`                                                | `tableSafety?`, per custom item `labelCheck?: string[]`                                                        | list header + `Check label` chip                                                           |

Cost: `check()` is regex over name + ingredients + steps, already run per recipe for `allergenWarnings`; the table is
loaded once per request. No caching needed at current scale; if p95 of `getForWeek` grows > 50 ms, memoise per
`(recipeId, tableHash)` in-process.

### 2.3 Plan shape (T-07.1)

Stored on `DietaryPreferences` (next to `mealsPerDay`, which it supersedes for new clients):

```prisma
planSlots      String[] @default([])   // [] = legacy: breakfast, lunch, dinner (+ engine snacks)
planDays       Int[]    @default([])   // [] = every day; 0 = Monday
timeCapMins    Int?                    // null = no limit (existing users: unchanged)
weekendNoLimit Boolean  @default(false)
cookingFor     Int?                    // 1 | 2; null = legacy (household decides)
leftovers      Boolean  @default(false) // persists "cook once, eat twice" (bug B-27)
```

```ts
// packages/types/src/plan-shape.ts (NEW)
export const planSlotSchema = z.enum(['breakfast', 'lunch', 'dinner', 'snack']);
export const planShapeSchema = z.object({
  slots: z.array(planSlotSchema).min(1).max(4),
  days: z.array(z.number().int().min(0).max(6)).min(1).max(7),
  timeCapMins: z.union([z.literal(15), z.literal(30), z.literal(45)]).nullable(),
  weekendNoLimit: z.boolean(),
  cookingFor: z.union([z.literal(1), z.literal(2)]).nullable(),
});
```

Every default reproduces today's plan, so an existing user who never opens the form gets the same week (UX-07 AC7).
The curated planner (`apps/api/src/application/meal-plan/curated-planner.ts` `planCuratedWeek` L93–179, today always
7 days × B/L/D + ≤ 2 snacks and ignoring `mealsPerDay`) gains a `shape` argument; AI generation receives the same
shape in its prompt (B-32 "no default 4th meal").

### 2.4 Jobs and landing (T-03.1, T-04.1)

`ChefProfile` gains `onboardingJobs OnboardingJob[] @default([])` (new enum `TRAIN | PLAN_MEALS | HOUSEHOLD |
USE_WHAT_I_HAVE | SAVED_RECIPES`), `trainingWeekdays Int[] @default([])` (the onboarding answer, used only until an
active routine has planned weekdays) and `showNutritionOnToday Boolean?` (null = derive from goal, B-31).
`preferences.setJobs` writes jobs **and** the legacy `onboardingIntent` (first mapped job), so web and old binaries
keep working; `preferences.setIntent` keeps working and back-fills `onboardingJobs` when empty. The **effective jobs**
rule (shared, `@chefer/utils` `effectiveJobs({ jobs, intent })`) maps legacy intents on read
(`EAT_BETTER → PLAN_MEALS`, `HOUSEHOLD → HOUSEHOLD`, `TRAIN → TRAIN`, `null → unknown`).

**Rev 2:** the enum gains **`TRACK`** ("Track what I eat": keeps the ring home, D20, and adds the Your targets
step). `effectiveJobs` also infers `TRACK` for users who logged on ≥ 3 of the last 7 days (the UX-03 pre-selection).
**Units follow typed values (bug B-43):** the pure `inferUnitsFromInput({ heightCm?, heightIn?, weightKg?, weightLb? })`
(`packages/utils/src/locale.ts`, next to the region guess at L54–60, L86–102) switches `preferredUnits` when the user
types metric values on an imperial default. It uses the existing `preferences.setDisplayPreferences` with a notice +
`Undo`. **Sunday auto-plan asked once (⚖ D-13):** `setJobs` accepts `autoPlanWeekly?: boolean` and writes the existing
`ChefProfile.autoPlanWeekly`.

Landing and home-card order are pure functions (`packages/utils/src/landing.ts`, `home-cards.ts`) fed by
`dashboard.summary` plus the persisted gym bootstrap (offline-safe), exactly as UX-04 specifies.

### 2.5 Week mechanics (T-08.1)

- **The B-13 data bug is wave 0 (T-00.15).** Four readers treat `findActiveWithDays` (`meal-plan.repository.ts`
  L309–315: newest ACTIVE plan by `createdAt`, any week) as "this week":
  - `mealPlan.getForWeek` offset 0 (`meal-plan.service.ts` L934–937);
  - `mealPlan.getActive`;
  - `shopping-list.service.ts` L356–359;
  - `dashboard.service.ts` L189.

  They all switch to one new repository method, `findForWeek(userId, weekStart)`, plus the existing carry-forward
  (`getForWeek` L945–968). The week start is the **local** Monday, computed from the client's `localDate` (§2.12);
  when that is absent, the API keeps today's server-date behaviour.

  `mealPlan.getActive` is kept for old clients and returns the current week's plan or `null`. It no longer falls back
  to next week. Each surface fires `plan_shown { surface, weekMatches }`.

  Rev 1 had these fixes spread across T-08.4 and T-04.2. They now land together in T-00.15, because the bug corrupts
  every Sunday planner's view.

- `defaultWeekOffset(now)` in **NEW** `packages/utils/src/week-default.ts` (Fri ≥ 15:00 → Sun = 1). Used by Plan and
  Shop on both platforms.
- **Shop fix (bug B-13)** is part of T-00.15 above; old clients already render `hasPlan: false`, so it is a bug fix,
  not a contract change.
- **Undo regenerate** needs no new procedure: `mealPlan.generate` returns an additive `previousPlanId: string | null`
  and the client calls the existing `mealPlan.restore({ planId })` (it already re-creates a plan as the newest row for
  its week, carrying ticks and custom items). **Undo replace** calls `replaceRecipe` with the previous `recipeId`.
- **Kept picks:** the slot JSON (`PlanMealSlotJson`, `meal-plan.repository.ts` L31–41, today `{ type, recipeId,
leftoverOf?, portion? }`) gains `pinned?: true` — JSON, no migration. `replaceRecipe` sets it; **NEW**
  `mealPlan.setSlotPinned` toggles it; `generate` gains `keepPinned?: boolean` (default **false** = today's behaviour
  for old clients; new clients send the confirm's switch value).
- **Price ranges:** the server keeps EUR point estimates; `priceRange()` / `formatPriceRange()` in **NEW**
  `packages/utils/src/price-range.ts` apply the ± band and the user's currency (existing client-side conversion in
  `packages/utils/src/currency.ts`). The band constant lives in one place (V5 tunes it).

### 2.6 Training days and the bump (T-06.1)

Training weekdays already flow from the active routine (`training-nutrition.service.ts` `trainingSchedule`
L176–179). The bump exists for **GAIN_MUSCLE only** (`packages/utils/src/training-nutrition.ts`
`hasTrainingDayBump` L60–62) and is applied only for premium (`buildTrainingDayNutrition` L159–183:
`applied = premium && isTrainingDay`; gates at `dashboard.service.ts` L197–202 and `tracker.service.ts` L157).

- ⚖ **D-2:** a new plan-feature key `trainingDayTargets` (free `true` if D-2 = yes) replaces `trainingNutrition` at
  those two gates; `trainingNutrition` keeps meaning "build the AI/curated week around training days". A key split,
  not a rename (old clients that read `trainingNutrition` for their lock copy keep working).
- **NEW** `mealPlan.getForWeek` output `trainingDays?: { dayOfWeek, dayName, kcalBonus, proteinBonus, done }[]`.
- Refuel snacks are **filtered server-side** (one filter, §2.1): `dashboard.summary` gains
  `refuelSnacks?: { id, name, proteinG, kcal, carbsG, fatG }[]` from the curated list in
  **NEW** `packages/utils/src/protein-snacks.ts`; the phone caches it with the summary, so the gym summary screen can
  show them offline.
- **Rev 2: weekday kinds (UX-06).** Each weekday is `lift` (from the routine), `run`, `long_run` or `rest`.
  - **Storage:** run kinds are stored on `ChefProfile.trainingDayKinds Json @default("{}")` (`{ "5": "long_run" }`),
    not on `GymProfile`, so a runner without a gym profile can have them. They are written by the new
    `training.setDayKinds`.
  - **Bumps:** `trainingDayBonus` gains a `kind` argument. `lift` keeps today's protein-led bump. `run` and `long_run`
    get carb-led bumps. `long_run` adds the pre-run snack idea the evening before. The numbers are for the dietitian
    (Q-3).
  - **Change log:** any change of kinds that moves a target goes through the change log (§2.11).

### 2.7 Premium mechanics (T-10.1)

`packages/utils/src/premium-activation.ts` exists (post-upgrade steps). **NEW** `packages/utils/src/premium-pitch.ts`
holds the job table (headline, lede, bullets), each bullet with `feature: PlanFeatureKey | 'planned'` and an
`available` flag derived from `PLAN_FEATURES` plus the flag set (§2.9), so a pitch can never promise an unbuilt
feature. The web nudge cap (`apps/web/src/features/premium/lib/nudge-cap.ts`) is split into a pure rule
(**NEW** `packages/utils/src/nudge-cap.ts`, storage-agnostic) and thin storage adapters (web `localStorage`, mobile
gym KV). Upgrade stays `user.upgradePlan` (no input); the PremiumSheet orchestrates the post-upgrade **action** on the
client (it calls `mealPlan.generate` for the right week etc.) — no new server procedure.

### 2.8 Health consent and the client-version gate (T-26.1, T-26.3)

Modelled on the AI consent (`User.aiDataConsentAt`, `user.grantAiDataConsent` / `revokeAiDataConsent`,
business_flow.md §25), with the same naming:

```prisma
// User — additive (a cache of the latest HEALTH ConsentEvent, read by the middleware without a join)
healthDataConsentAt      DateTime?   // null = not given / withdrawn
healthDataConsentVersion String?     // copy version shown when given (e.g. "2026-10-hc1")
```

**Rev 2:** the source of truth for every consent is the `ConsentEvent` log (§2.13). A grant or withdrawal writes an
event **and** refreshes the cache column in one transaction. `healthDataWithdrawnAt` from rev 1 is dropped: the
withdrawal event is the audit trail.

Procedures (all `protectedProcedure`, in the new `privacy` router of §2.13): `privacy.grantHealthConsent({ version })`,
`privacy.withdrawHealthData({ confirm: 'WITHDRAW' })` (deletes allergies/diets/dislikes on `DietaryPreferences` and every `HouseholdMember`,
goal, body metrics and own targets on `ChefProfile`, all `WeightEntry` rows; logs the withdrawal event; keeps the account),
and `user.me` returns `healthDataConsentAt` (additive, like `aiDataConsentAt`).

**The gate must not break installed binaries (C5d).** UX-26 AC1 ("API rejects health writes without a recorded
consent") would make every old binary's `preferences.updateSafety`, `household.add/update`,
`preferences.saveProfileBasics`, `tracker.logWeight`… fail. Design:

1. **Client capability header.** New clients send `x-chefer-api-level: <n>` on every tRPC request (mobile: in
   `apps/mobile/src/lib/trpc-links.ts` headers; web: the tRPC client headers). The API reads it in context creation
   (`apps/api/src/lib/trpc.ts`) as `ctx.clientApiLevel` (absent = 0). Level 1 = "understands the health-consent
   error and shows the sheet".
2. **Enforcement mode** from env (`HEALTH_CONSENT_ENFORCE=off|declared|all`, default `off`; validated in
   `apps/api/src/lib/env.ts`): `off` records nothing; `declared` rejects un-consented health writes **only** from
   clients with `clientApiLevel ≥ 1` (typed `PRECONDITION_FAILED` with `cause: 'HEALTH_CONSENT_REQUIRED'`);
   `all` rejects from every client. Old binaries are never rejected under `declared`.
3. **Rollout:** ship API (`off`) → ship web + OTA (level 1) → flip to `declared` → once the owner confirms every
   installed binary has taken the OTA (the More footer shows the update id), flip to `all`. A store build must
   never ship before `declared` is live.
4. Enforcement is middleware (**NEW** `requireHealthConsent` in `apps/api/src/lib/trpc.ts`, composed onto the
   health-write procedures), not `if` checks in handlers (CLAUDE.md rule 5).

The same header gives future breaking-change work a versioning hook (the "explicit versioning plan" CLAUDE.md
requires).

### 2.9 Feature flags (T-00.8)

There is no flag mechanism today. Minimal, typed, server-owned:

- **NEW** `packages/types/src/feature-flags.ts`: `featureFlagsSchema = z.object({ trainingBumpFree: z.boolean(),
householdFirstWeekFree: z.boolean(), servingsTwoFree: z.boolean(), ownTargetsFree: z.boolean(),
budgetFree: z.boolean(), structuredLinkImportFree: z.boolean(), … }).partial()` — every key optional; a missing
  key means **off**.
- Values come from one env var `FEATURE_FLAGS` (comma list of enabled keys) parsed in `apps/api/src/lib/env.ts`
  (C3), exposed by **NEW** `profile.flags` (public query, cached by clients like `profile.aiProviders`). Server
  behaviour reads the same parsed set (`apps/api/src/lib/flags.ts`, **NEW**).
- Clients treat a failed or absent `profile.flags` as all-off, so old APIs and old clients behave as today.
- ⚖ decisions map to flags: D-2 → `trainingBumpFree`, `ownTargetsFree` (UX-35, Now), `budgetFree`, `pantryEntryFree` (Next); D-6 → `structuredLinkImportFree`;
  D-7 → `householdFirstWeekFree`, `servingsTwoFree`. Flipping a flag is an env change + API restart
  (`infrastructure/scripts/env.sh`), no deploy.

### 2.10 Shared copy and lint (T-00.6)

Stage 3 Appendix A's modules are created in wave 0 (empty exports first, filled by lanes). A **NEW** ESLint rule in
`packages/config/eslint` (`chefer/no-forbidden-safety-copy`) scans string literals in `**/safety-copy.ts`,
`**/features/safety/**`, `premium-pitch.ts` and `wellness-copy.ts` for `safe`, `allergen-free`, `nut-free`,
`guaranteed`, `suitable for`, `AI meal plans tailored`, `nutrition profile`, `diabetics`, `cure`, `treat`,
`prevent`. A Vitest in `packages/utils` asserts the same over the exported strings (belt and braces; ESLint rules on
string content are easy to bypass with template literals).

### 2.11 Targets: one resolver, own overrides, and a change log (T-35.1, T-11.1)

- **One resolver.** `resolveDailyTargets` (`preferences.service.ts` L177–222) returns
  `{ effective, suggested, source: 'own' | 'suggested', inputs }`:
  - `suggested` is today's computation, including the lifter g/kg rule (`training-nutrition.service.ts` L122–136),
    the coach dial and the training-day bonus by kind.
  - `effective` is the user's own numbers when `ChefProfile.targetMode = OWN`, else `suggested`.
  - `inputs` are the values the explanation sentences name: weight, height, age, activity, goal, lifter, rate, BMI
    rule.

  Every consumer reads `effective`: Today, the tracker, Plan day targets, curated and AI generation, the coach
  review and Progress. The legacy `ChefProfile.dailyCalorieTarget` is written with the effective value, so old
  clients read the right number.

- **Sanity rules** live in `packages/utils/src/training-nutrition.ts`:
  - BMI ≥ 30 uses an adjusted body weight for protein;
  - no `proteinGapG` or "add a snack" for a Lose goal (rev 1 T-22.4, kept);
  - no portion above 2× to chase a target — the day is flagged instead;
  - the 1,200 kcal floor stays.
- **"Never change it silently" is enforced by detection, not by hooks.**
  - **Snapshot.** `ChefProfile.targetSnapshot Json?` stores the last `{ effective, suggested, inputs }` the user
    saw.
  - **Detection.** Every read of the targets (`dashboard.summary`, `tracker.getDay`, `targets.get`) recomputes them.
    When `effective` differs from the snapshot, or `suggested` differs by ≥ 5 % for an OWN user, the server writes
    a `TargetChange` row (`fields[{ field, before, after }]`, `reason`, `kind: CHANGED | SUGGESTED`).
  - **Reason.** The reason is inferred from which inputs changed: `GYM_SETUP` (lifter flag), `WEIGHT`, `GOAL`,
    `DAY_KIND`, `COACH`.
  - **Resolution.** The snapshot moves on. For a CHANGED row, the new value applies at once and
    `targets.acknowledgeChange({ id, keep: true })` writes the old value back as the user's own. For a SUGGESTED
    row, nothing applies until the user accepts.
  - **Why detection.** Every current and future change source is caught (gym setup in L-GYM's files, weight logs in
    L-TRACK's, kinds in L-GYM's, the coach in L-TRACK's) without cross-lane hooks. That is the file-ownership reason
    to prefer it over "call the recorder from each writer".
- **The coach proposes, never overwrites.**
  - `coach.service.ts` L210 stops adding to `targetAdjustmentKcal`. The review stores its proposal on
    `ChefReview.proposedAdjustmentKcal`, and the proposal surfaces as a SUGGESTED change; accepting it applies the
    dial. The existing `ChefReview.adjustmentKcal` keeps meaning "applied", now 0 until accepted.
  - The review prompt text changes from "adjusted by" to "suggests" (`prompts.ts` L450–465), so old clients' banners
    stay true.
- **Goals.** Two enum values are added: `Goal.RECOMP` and `Goal.PERFORMANCE`.
  - **Compat:** old clients render the goal from `preferences.get` against a fixed `GOALS` list
    (`src/features/preferences/types.ts`), so for requests without `x-chefer-api-level ≥ 1` the API serialises
    `RECOMP → MAINTAIN` and `PERFORMANCE → MAINTAIN` in `chefProfile.goal`. The true value is in the new `goalV2`.
  - Every `switch (goal)` in `apps/api` and `packages/utils` becomes exhaustive (the `assertNever` in
    `packages/utils/src/assert.ts`).

### 2.12 One local-day contract (T-21.1)

- **What changes.** Every procedure that means "a day" accepts the device's `localDate` (`YYYY-MM-DD`); most already
  do (`dashboard.summary`, `tracker.*`, `gym.bootstrap`). New optional inputs:
  - `tracker.weeklySummary` and `tracker.monthlySummary` (bug B-33: `tracker.service.ts` L383–392 uses `setUTCDate`);
  - the chat body (bug B-06);
  - `mealPlan.getForWeek` / `shoppingList.getForWeek` (`localDate`, to compute the local Monday for T-00.15).
- **Time zone.** It is stored once on `ChefProfile.timeZone String?` (IANA, sent by `setDisplayPreferences`) for
  server-initiated work: the weekly worker and the quiet-days nudge text.
- **Guard.** A grep-based lint in CI (**NEW** `scripts/check-utc-days.mjs`) fails on
  `new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]` / `slice(0, 10)` in `apps/api/src/application/**`, except in files on an
  allowlist.

### 2.13 Consent log, emails and export (T-39.1–T-39.5)

- **NEW** `ConsentEvent` table:
  - fields: `id`, `userId`, `kind`, `granted`, `providers String[]`, `documentVersion String?`, `source`, `createdAt`;
  - `kind` is an enum: `TERMS | PRIVACY | AGE | AI | ANALYTICS_ANON | ANALYTICS_LINKED | EMAIL_WEEK_READY |
EMAIL_RECAP | AUTO_PLAN | HEALTH`;
  - `source` is `web | mobile | migration`;
  - it is append-only and never updated.
- **Every consent write logs through `ConsentService.record()`:** `user.grantAiDataConsent` / `revokeAiDataConsent`
  (unchanged signatures), `notifications.setEmailPreferences`, `preferences.setAutoPlanWeekly`,
  `auth.register` (new optional `acceptedTermsVersion`, `ageConfirmed`), the analytics switches (a new
  `privacy.recordAnalyticsConsent({ anonymous, linked })`, because analytics consent is device-local and the log is
  what makes it provable) and health consent.
- **Backfill.** A boot-time, idempotent job writes a `source: migration` AI event for every `aiDataConsentAt` that
  is not null. `User.aiDataConsentAt` stays as a cache for old clients.
- **Email defaults.** `User.weeklyEmailReady` / `weeklyEmailRecap` and `ChefProfile.autoPlanWeekly` get
  `@default(false)`. A default change applies to **new rows only** under `db push` (Postgres `ALTER COLUMN … SET
DEFAULT`), so existing accounts keep their values. Registration writes the three values explicitly, not by
  default, and logs them. Existing accounts get a one-time card, tracked by the new `User.emailDefaultsNoticeAt`.
- **Export.** `exportAccountData` (`account-data.service.ts` L11) adds the consent log, email preferences, the AI
  call log (type, provider, time — no model output; `AiCallLog` has no provider column today, so it gains
  `provider String?`, filled from the AI route going forward) and shopping lists + pantry. It returns
  `{ filename: 'chefer-export-YYYY-MM-DD.json', json }` from a new `privacy.exportData`; `user.exportData` is kept
  for old clients.

### 2.14 Native release: what needs one, and why (decision)

Stage 3 §7.1 puts **one native release in wave 4** with (a) the PostHog RN SDK for UX-12 and (b) `expo-file-system`

- `expo-sharing` for UX-39's file export. Rev 1 of this plan chose a pure-JS PostHog transport and no native
  rebuild. After checking the binaries (§1 facts):

| Capability                                         | Native module needed?                                                                                                                                                                    | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| UX-12 analytics                                    | **No** with the JS transport (§5.10); the RN SDK would add device/storage modules                                                                                                        | **JS transport, OTA, wave 1.** It meets UX-12's privacy-by-default constraints more simply: no device identifiers, no disk. Only session replay or flags would justify the SDK, and both are out of scope. |
| UX-39 export: write the file                       | `expo-file-system` — **already linked** (`ExpoFileSystem` 57.0.6 in `Podfile.lock`, a dependency of `expo`); pnpm needs it as a **direct** dependency of `apps/mobile` at exactly 57.0.6 | OTA, **if** `runtimeversion:resolve` still matches `release-builds/runtime-*.txt` after adding the dependency. The ladder step 3f checks it; if the fingerprint moves, it joins the native release.        |
| UX-39 export: share the file                       | iOS: RN `Share.share({ url: fileUri })` shares a file with no new module. Android: RN `Share` shares text only, so a file needs **`expo-sharing`** (not in the binary)                   | **Native release, wave 4, containing `expo-sharing`** (+ `expo-file-system` pinned). Until then (OTA): iOS gets the named file, Android gets the titled text (stage 3's fallback).                         |
| Gym CSV export (`export-row.tsx` L30, text today)  | same as above                                                                                                                                                                            | Rides the same release (named `.csv` file).                                                                                                                                                                |
| UX-36 reminders, quiet nudge, rest-timer rationale | expo-notifications — present                                                                                                                                                             | OTA                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| UX-39 in-app legal screen                          | react-native-webview — present                                                                                                                                                           | OTA                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| UX-21.16 camera-denied → Photos / Settings         | expo-image-picker, RN `Linking` — present                                                                                                                                                | OTA                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| PAT-10 time picker                                 | JS                                                                                                                                                                                       | OTA (the native picker alternative stays unscheduled)                                                                                                                                                      |

**Rebuild list: exactly one native release (wave 4), containing `expo-sharing` and a pinned direct
`expo-file-system`.** `react-native-gesture-handler` (approved by the owner for the motion workstream) may ride along
if that workstream is ready (Q-17); nothing else. The discrepancy with stage 3 (SDK in the release) is recorded in
both documents' changelogs.

---

## 3. Prisma schema changes and migrations

All changes are **additive** so that production's `prisma db push --skip-generate` applies them without
`--accept-data-loss` (§1). Each lane that owns a change also runs `pnpm db:migrate` locally to add a named migration
to `packages/database/prisma/migrations/` (keeps the history honest for a future switch to `migrate deploy`), runs
`pnpm db:generate`, restarts the tsx-watch API (the Prisma client is cached), and updates `infrastructure.md` §6.

| --- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

Null + any stored term that is not a canonical label ⇒ `tableSafety.needsReview`.                                                                                                                                         |

(≤ 500), rulesSnapshot Json, createdAt`; indexes on `userId`, `recipeId`; cascade on user                                                                                                                                                                                                          | T-01.5                         | 0                         | In `exportData`; the cascade covers `deleteAccount`.                                                                                                                                                                            |

| S12 | **NEW** `TargetChange` _(rev 2)_ | `id, userId, kind (CHANGED\|SUGGESTED), reason String, fields Json, createdAt, resolvedAt DateTime?, resolution String?` (`USE_NEW\|KEEP_OLD\|AUTO`); index `[userId, resolvedAt]`                                                                                                                                                                      | T-11.1                         | 0                         | Auto-resolves after 14 days (lazy, on read). Included in `exportData`.                                                                                                                                                          |
| S13 | `ChefReview` _(rev 2)_           | `+ proposedAdjustmentKcal Int?`, `+ proposalResolvedAt DateTime?`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | T-35.4                         | 0                         | New reviews propose; old rows are unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| S14 | **NEW** `ConsentEvent` _(rev 2)_ | `id, userId, kind ConsentKind, granted Boolean, providers String[] @default([]), documentVersion String?, source String, createdAt`; index `[userId, createdAt]`                                                                                                                                                                                        | T-39.2                         | 0                         | Idempotent boot backfill from `aiDataConsentAt` (`source: migration`).                                                                                                                                                          |
| S15 | `User` _(rev 2)_                 | `weeklyEmailReady` / `weeklyEmailRecap` **default → false** (new rows only), `+ emailDefaultsNoticeAt DateTime?`, `+ termsAcceptedVersion String?` (cache)                                                                                                                                                                                              | T-39.3, T-39.1                 | 0                         | Existing rows keep their values (`db push` changes the column default only). Verify on a prod-dump restore (`infrastructure/scripts/restore-dump.sh`) that no existing value flips.                                             |
| S16 | `ChefProfile` _(rev 2)_          | `autoPlanWeekly` **default → false** (new rows only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | T-39.3                         | 0                         | Same check as S15. Risk: an existing user with **no** ChefProfile row gets `false` when the row is first created later (registration creates the row, so this should be rare) — count such users on the dump before the deploy. |
| S17 | `AiCallLog` _(rev 2)_            | `+ provider String?`; `+ saved Boolean?`; enum `AiCallType + CURATED_PLAN` (imports: counted as "used" only when saved — display only, see Q-18)                                                                                                                                                                                                        | T-39.5, T-10.8                 | 0                         | Null for historic rows.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

**Deliberately not schema:** kept picks (`pinned?` inside `MealPlanDay.meals` JSON), `previousPlanId` (derived),
week coverage (derived from `firstShoppingDay`), safety notes (derived from stored strings), analytics consent
(device-local, like web's per-browser choice), feature flags (env), AI-generated marker (`Recipe.source === 'AI'`
already exists), log-entry ids (`entryId` inside `DailyLog.loggedMeals` JSON), derived diet tags (computed), the
saved-for-later workout (the existing on-device active-session store, 24 h).

**Enum additions** (`OnboardingJob` incl. `TRACK`, `TargetMode`, `ConsentKind`, `Goal.RECOMP` / `Goal.PERFORMANCE`) are safe
under `db push`; no enum value is removed or renamed. **All Now schema lands in wave 0** (T-00.10), so no lane edits
`schema.prisma` during a wave.

---

## 4. API surface changes (procedure ledger)

Every row is additive. "Old clients" = the embedded bundles of installed binaries and any not-yet-updated OTA.
The owning task adds the row to `infrastructure.md` §8 and the flow to `business_flow.md` in the same PR.

### 4.1 New procedures

Rev 2 moves some rev-1 names into new, single-owner routers, so that no two lanes edit one router file in the same
wave (§7.3). None of these procedures has shipped yet, so renaming them costs nothing.

| Procedure                                                                     | Access                                                  | Input (Zod sketch, `@chefer/types`)                                                                                                                                                                         | Output                                                                                | Task                   |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------- |

| `mealPlan.setSlotPinned`                                                      | protected                                               | `{ planId, dayOfWeek: 0–6, mealType, slotIndex?, pinned: z.boolean() }`                                                                                                                                     | `{ ok: true }`                                                                        | T-07.4                 |
| `mealPlan.scaleDay` _(rev 2)_                                                 | protected                                               | `{ planId, dayOfWeek, factor: z.number().min(0.75).max(1.5), preview: z.boolean() }`                                                                                                                        | `{ day: DayPlanDto, totals }` (preview does not persist)                              | T-11.3                 |
| `mealPlan.suggestSwap`                                                        | protected, AI quota                                     | as `swapRecipe` minus commit                                                                                                                                                                                | `{ proposal }` — **optional**, only if Q-6 approves; fallback is commit + Undo        | T-08.6                 |
| `preferences.setJobs`                                                         | protected                                               | `{ jobs: z.array(onboardingJobSchema).min(1).max(6), trainingWeekdays?: int[0–6] ≤ 7, autoPlanWeekly?: z.boolean() }`                                                                                       | `{ jobs, intent }` (writes the legacy `onboardingIntent` too; logs AUTO_PLAN consent) | T-03.1                 |

New router files: `apps/api/src/routers/{safety,targets,training,privacy}.router.ts`, each with its application
service (`application/{safety,targets,training-days,privacy}/…`), registered in `routers/index.ts` by W0-B as stubs.

### 4.3 Removed or renamed

None. `preferences.setup` (premium, uncapped arrays) keeps its shape; new clients stop calling it from onboarding
(UX-03 collapses premium onboarding into the same builder and writes through `saveProfileBasics`,
`updateSafety`, `mealPlan.setShape`, `targets.set`, `setJobs`). Its arrays get the same caps as `safetySchema` **only** for requests that
declare `x-chefer-api-level ≥ 1` (C5d).

## 5. Tasks per UX item

**How to read a task row.** `ID · task · files (NEW = new file) · size · ship`. "Ship" is **OTA** (JS only),
**API** (server only, deploys on push), or **Native**. Below each table: **Tests**, **Web parity**, **Docs**,
**Flags**, **Analytics**, **Risk**. Mobile paths are under `apps/mobile/`, web paths under `apps/web/src/`, API paths
under `apps/api/src/` unless written in full.

  `tagConflicts`. Contract (`apps/mobile/tests/contract/safety.contract.test.ts`, NEW): `safety.getTable`,
  `safety.report` then `recipe.list({ forTable: true })` excludes the recipe (AC10), `safety.getTable` reports
  the recognition of legacy strings. Jest: `safety-picker.test.tsx` (read-back within one render of selection, vegan greys
  dairy-free, "Something else" outcomes, 44 pt), `household-editor.test.tsx` (You card first, summary line),
  `recipe-form.test.tsx` (tags round-trip). Maestro NEW `e2e/safety.flow.yaml`: set Tree nuts, generate a curated
  week, assert no `Greek Yogurt Parfait` (AC3); open a recipe → Report → snackbar. Playwright: extend
  `tests/e2e/` with `safety.spec.ts` (web onboarding diet step read-back, Replace sheet hidden count).

  once.

  `recipe.list` input/outputs), §6 (S2, S3);
  `business_flow.md` §9 (filtering in generation), §13 (chat), §16 (import), §17 (household dislikes now hard) and a
  **NEW §27 "Safety filter & reporting flow"**.
- **Flags.** None (safety is never flagged off). The member-dislikes-become-hard change ships unflagged but is
  called out in the release notes.
- **Analytics.** `safety_readback_viewed`, `safety_conflict_shown`, `safety_issue_reported`,
  `safety_migration_resolved` (typed in T-12.1; enum/count properties only).
- **Risk.** **High** (liability). (1) Over-blocking empties pools: `MIN_SAFE_POOL_SIZE = 3`
  (`curated-recipes/index.ts` L79) already returns a PRECONDITION_FAILED — T-10.4 turns it into the "Pick recipes
  yourself" card; the regression report lists pool sizes per profile so content gaps (vegan + coeliac) are visible.
  (2) Taxonomy content needs the dietitian review (V1) before the store release, not before merge. (3) Member
  dislikes becoming hard may shrink household pools — measured by the regression report. **Validate:** V1.

  - 80×8 → next ≥ 90 kg with the "Tough day at 90 kg" sentence; deadlift 150×5 on 1 of 3 (prescribed 40×10) → next
    starts from 150); property test: next target ≥ heaviest completed working set unless a stated miss rule;
    `reasons.test.ts`: no `today` in `when: 'next'` output (AC2); `todayStatus` cases. Contract:
    `gym-engine.contract.test.ts` — server fold equals client `applyFinishedSession` for the worked examples
    (engine parity); `completeSetup({ weeklyGoal: 5 })` stores 5. Jest: `gym-workout.test.tsx` (reps propagate, no
    collapse), `gym-workout-summary.test.tsx` (40 → 150 kg in ≤ 5 taps), `gym-settings.test.tsx` (19:30 in ≤ 3 taps),
    `gym-stats.test.tsx` (label `255.4 kg` not clipped), `gym-today.test.tsx` (done/rest). Maestro: all five
    `e2e/gym-*.flow.yaml` green on iOS **and** Pixel_8; `gym-setup.flow.yaml` extended for the split question.
    Playwright: `tests/e2e/gym.spec.ts` for web parity pieces.
- **Web parity.** Web gym exists (G5: `app/(dashboard)/gym/{setup,summary/[id],stats,settings,workout,routine}`,
  `features/gym/**`). The engine fix (T-05.1) lands on web automatically (shared utils; web's
  `summary-view.tsx:338` passes `'next'`). Same PR group: setup split question (`features/gym/setup/setup-wizard.tsx`),
  weekly goal, settings equipment/vest (`features/gym/settings/settings-view.tsx`, already `<input type="time">`),
  typed Adjust (`summary-view.tsx`), tappable names, stats labels (`features/gym/stats/*`), today done/rest
  (`features/gym/today/today-view.tsx`). Anything not yet on web gets a reverse ledger row.
- **Docs.** §6 (S6), §8 (`completeSetup` input, `profile.save` field), `business_flow.md` §21 (progression rules,
  weekly goal) and §22.
- **Flags.** None. The engine change is not flaggable (both engines must agree); it is gated by tests.
- **Analytics.** `split_chosen`, `suggestion_overridden` (existing), `gym_settings_opened { from }`,
  `glossary_opened { term }`.
- **Risk.** **High for trust, medium for engineering.** (1) Engine parity: the phone folds offline with the engine in
  its bundle; ship API first, then OTA; an old bundle's optimistic next target may differ until the next
  `gym.bootstrap` (server wins). (2) The back-off heuristic without a set type can misclassify a deliberate
  heavier-then-lighter pyramid — the property tests must include pyramids and ascending sets. (3) Protect D1/D2:
  snapshot diffs of set rows and the Why? sheet must be empty. **Validate:** V7.

### 5.10 UX-12 Mobile analytics with consent (L-DATA, wave 1)

**Technical decision: ship over OTA with a pure-JS transport, not the PostHog React Native SDK.** Stage 3 assumed
the SDK (which pulls native modules: device/app info, file storage) and therefore a native rebuild. The web already
runs PostHog cookieless (`persistence: 'memory'`, `apps/web/src/lib/analytics.ts`). The mobile equivalent needs only
PostHog's public HTTP capture endpoint (`https://eu.i.posthog.com/batch/`), an in-memory queue, `fetch`,
`Platform` and `expo-constants` (already installed). That is about 150 lines of JS, keeps the fingerprint
unchanged, and matches the spec's "in-memory session id, nothing on disk" exactly. Adopting the SDK later (session
replay, feature flags) remains possible in a planned native release (Q-9).

| ID     | Task                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Files                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Size | Ship |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- | ---- |
| T-12.1 | **Shared `EventMap`** with a type-level health-data guard: property types restricted to `number \| boolean \| <string-literal union> \| <literal>[]`; a `tsd`-style Vitest (`expectTypeOf`) fails on any bare `string`; includes stage 2 §7.3 events + the existing gym `GymEventMap` (`src/features/gym/analytics.ts` L13–36) + web funnel events                                                     | NEW `packages/types/src/analytics-events.ts` (+ `.test-d.ts` / Vitest type test); web `lib/analytics.ts` `capture` (L121) becomes `capture<E extends keyof EventMap>(…)`                                                                                                                               | S    | —    |

  are updated in the **same release** (checklist item in wave 4). Default of the anonymous switch: ⚖ Q-8 (opt-in is
  acceptable to the strategy).

  UX-39, not an analytics SDK.

  events); `mobile_parity_backlog.md` row of 2026-09-26 (analytics) → `done`.

  events" backs it. ⚖ counsel on anonymous-on by default (Q-8). **No native rebuild.**
