- **Tests.** API: gym setup completion for a user at 128 g protein → one `TargetChange { GYM_SETUP, 128 → 93 }`;
  `acknowledgeChange({ keep: true })` sets OWN with 128 and the ring reads 128 (AC1); OWN user + weight change → a
  SUGGESTED row, and `effective` is unchanged (AC2); the first read after deploy writes a baseline without a row;
  `scaleDay` preview does not persist; no `proteinGapG` for Lose; BMI 32 → adjusted weight (AC5). Utils: every
  `explain-targets` sentence pattern. Jest: the notice renders as an alert with before → after; tapping the ring and
  macro labels opens the sheet (AC3); the chip is never amber on first render (AC4). Maestro NEW
  `e2e/targets.flow.yaml` (complete gym setup → notice → Keep → ring). Playwright: dashboard notice + explain.

  `business_flow.md` §10 and a NEW §29 "Targets: own, suggested and change notices".
- **Analytics.** `target_change_notice_viewed { reason, kept }`, `number_why_opened { metric }`,
  `plan_miss_fixed { action }`.
- **Risk.** Medium. Detection-on-read is simple and complete, but a noisy input (daily weigh-ins) could produce a
  notice a day. Mitigate: only ≥ 5 % changes (or ≥ 5 g protein / 50 kcal) create a row, and weight-driven changes are
  debounced to one per 7 days. The coach no longer auto-applies, which changes the weekly-review behaviour for premium
  users; the review text must say "suggests" (§2.11).

### 5.17 Later (one line each)

- **UX-24 Romanian UI:** i18n runtime (`i18next` or a tiny `t()` over the `copy.ts` modules), RO strings, a language
  row in Settings; store listing RO. XL. OTA (no native module needed if `Intl` suffices; `expo-localization`
  would be native).
- **UX-27 Household co-access:** identity + sharing: `HouseholdMembership` (user ↔ owner household, role), invite
  tokens, shared plan/list reads scoped by household, live ticks (the list toggle is already SERIALIZABLE with retry,
  `shoppingList.toggleItems`), permissions middleware. XL. API + OTA. Needs its own design doc.
- **UX-38 Apple Health / Health Connect sync (⚖ D-12, out of scope for now):** would need `react-native-health` /
  `react-native-health-connect` (native modules → a native release, HealthKit entitlement, Play health-permissions
  declaration) and health-data consent (T-26). The cheap half — one shared body weight — is UX-20 (Next).

## 7. Execution plan for agents

### 7.1 Reconciliation with stage 3 §7 (rev 2)

The final UX packaging (stage 3 §7, rebuilt) is adopted: **wave 0** has 3 groups, and W0-C now carries the B-13 data
bug; **wave 1** has 6 lanes (new **L-TRACK** for UX-19, UX-35 and the core of UX-11); **wave 2** has 4 lanes;
**wave 3** has 2; **wave 4** is integration. Stage 3 leaves the server split to this stage. The deviations below each
have a technical reason.

| #   | Deviation from the UX packaging                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Why                                                                                                                                                       |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| R1  | **All Now schema, repository ports, the new router stubs (`safety`, `targets`, `training`, `privacy`), both barrel `index.ts` files and every new gym-KV key land in wave 0** (T-00.7, T-00.10)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Otherwise `schema.prisma`, `routers/index.ts`, the barrels and `offline/keys.ts` would be edited by 4–6 lanes in one wave.                                |
| R2  | **Server hot files have one owner per wave** (§7.3). `meal-plan.service.ts`: W0-C → L-PLAN → L-SAFE2 → L-PLAN2. `preferences.router/service`: L-TRACK → L-HOME → L-CONSENT. `recipe.service` + `favourite-recipe.repository`: L-SAFE in wave 1 (so the server side of T-08.10, dedupe/slot/exclude, and T-BUG-11 are done by L-SAFE; L-PLAN keeps the picker UI). `tracker.service`: L-TRACK (so T-21.1's tracker part is L-TRACK's; the chat part and `progress.tsx` stay L-ENTRY's). | Several lanes need each of these files; the UX map only covers client files.                                                                              |
| R3  | **New procedures live in new routers** (`mealPlan.getShape/setShape`, `targets.*`, `training.*`, `privacy.*`) rather than stage 3's `preferences.setTargets` / `acknowledgeTargetChange` / `privacy.getConsentHistory` names                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | This keeps `preferences.router.ts` single-owner. None of these names has shipped.                                                                         |
| R4  | **"Never change silently" is detected on read** (§2.11), not hooked into each writer                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Gym setup (L-GYM), weight (L-TRACK), kinds (L-GYM, L-PLAN2) and the coach (L-TRACK) would otherwise all have to call L-TRACK's recorder in the same wave. |
| R5  | **The change-notice card moves to `src/features/nutrition/`** (L-TRACK); L-HOME places it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Wave 1 then touches no file in L-HOME's directories.                                                                                                      |
| R6  | **"Build, then place"** (from rev 1): wave-1 lanes export components (MigrationCard, HowYouCookCard, TargetsCard, ChangeNoticeCard, PlanMissSheet, Resume/paused workout card, missed-day card, analytics card); the owner of `app/(food)/index.tsx`, `app/preferences.tsx` or `app/profile.tsx` places them in the next wave                                                                                                                                                          | Avoids edits to the busiest screens within one wave.                                                                                                      |
| R7  | **Native release: `expo-sharing` only**, not the PostHog SDK (§2.14)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | The JS transport covers UX-12, and the file-system module is already in the binary.                                                                       |
| R8  | The chat guardrail prompt is a W0-C hotfix (T-00.14)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | It is a compliance floor, and `prompts.ts` belongs to L-PLAN in wave 1.                                                                                   |
| R9  | **The safety regression suite and the UTC-day check are required CI jobs** from the moment they land                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | They are the B-01 target, the B-26 evidence trail and the B-06/B-33 bug class.                                                                            |
| R10 | **Each wave merges to `master`** (API deploys, then the OTA auto-publishes)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Keeps the API ahead of the JS and each release small (from rev 1; ⚖ Q-13).                                                                                |

### 7.2 Waves and lanes

```
Wave 0  (≈ 4–5 days)  W0-A kit & patterns · W0-B contracts, schema, routers, nav, flags · W0-C hotfixes (B-34/B-46, B-13 data, B-31 interim, B-10, chat guardrail) ── merge → master
Wave 1  (≈ 2 weeks)   L-SAFE · L-GYM · L-PLAN · L-ENTRY · L-TRACK · L-DATA ───────────────────────────────────── merge → master
Wave 2  (≈ 1.5 weeks) L-HOME · L-MONEY · L-SAFE2 · L-GYM (cont.) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── merge → master
Wave 3  (≈ 1 week)    L-PLAN2 · L-CONSENT ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── merge → master
Wave 4  (≈ 1 week)    Integrate & verify · ONE native release (expo-sharing + pinned expo-file-system) · store privacy answers · listing · counsel · HEALTH_CONSENT_ENFORCE=declared
Legal/counsel track (owner) from day 1 — deadline 9 Dec 2026.
```

**Docs:** each lane edits only its rows/sections of `infrastructure.md` and `business_flow.md` (§8), and markdown
conflicts are resolved at the wave merge. `mobile_parity_backlog.md` is append-only for any lane. `apps/mobile/e2e/**`:
the lane that owns a screen owns its flows.

### 7.4 Worktrees and branches

- Integration branch: none long-lived — each **wave** has `integrate/ux-now-w<N>` cut from `master`; lanes branch
  from it and PR into it; after the ladder passes it merges to `master` (R10).
- Lane branches: `feat/ux-now/<lane>` — `w0-kit`, `w0-contracts`, `w0-hotfix`, `safe`, `gym`, `plan`, `entry`,
  `data`, `track` (rev 2), `home`, `money`, `safe2`, `plan2`, `consent`; wave 4's native release on `release/native-1`.
- Worktrees: `../chefer-<lane>` (the repo's existing pattern, e.g. `../chefer-s1`), each with its own gitignored
  `apps/api/.env` on a distinct `PORT` (3011 + lane index) and its own database name (`chefer_<lane>`) so contract
  tests don't collide. Metro stays on :8083 — only one lane at a time runs Maestro on a given simulator; Android
  (`Pixel_8`) and iOS simulators can serve two lanes in parallel.
- Commits: Conventional Commits with the task id in the body (`feat(api): one safety filter service (T-01.2)`).
- No lane pushes to `master` directly; W0-C hotfixes go through a PR to `master` with the same ladder minus Maestro
  (they are small), then are merged into the wave-1 integration branch.

### 7.5 Verification ladder (per lane PR, and in full per wave merge)

| Level | Command / check                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Lane PR             | Wave merge          |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------- | ------------------- |
| 0     | `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint` (incl. the forbidden-copy rule)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | ✔                   | ✔                   |
| 1     | `pnpm test` (Vitest in `packages/*`, `apps/api`, `apps/web`; Jest in `apps/mobile/tests/unit`) — every new pure function has tests                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | ✔                   | ✔                   |
| 1s    | **Safety regression suite** + report diff (0 failures; pool sizes per profile recorded)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | from T-01.1 on      | ✔                   |
| 2     | `pnpm mobile:contract` (local API + DB) — new/changed procedures                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | ✔                   | ✔                   |
| 2c    | **Installed-binary compatibility run**: the contract tests **and** `tests/e2e` API calls from the last released commit (`git worktree add ../chefer-compat <last-master-tag>`) run against the new API — proves C5                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | API-touching lanes  | ✔                   |
| 3     | `pnpm --filter @chefer/mobile bundle:check` (`expo export`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | ✔                   | ✔                   |
| 3f    | **Runtime fingerprint unchanged**: `npx expo-updates runtimeversion:resolve --platform ios\|android` equals `apps/mobile/release-builds/runtime-<platform>.txt` (the same check `apps/mobile/scripts/publish-update.sh` runs before publishing) — a change means installed binaries would not receive the OTA; schedule a native release. **Rev 2:** the only planned change is wave 4's (`expo-sharing`); L-DATA's pinned `expo-file-system` must pass this check or move to wave 4 | ✔                   | ✔                   |
| 4     | Maestro: the lane's flows on iOS (`e2e/run-suite.sh <udid>`) **and** Android `Pixel_8`; read the screenshots on failure                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | layout lanes        | full suite, both OS |
| 5     | Playwright: `cd tests && pnpm exec playwright test` + `--project=mobile` sweep for any web layout change                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | web-touching lanes  | ✔                   |
| 6     | Acceptance criteria of the lane's UX specs listed in the PR with evidence (test names, screenshots at default and 1.8× text, iOS + Pixel_8)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | ✔                   | —                   |
| 7     | Protected delights: empty snapshot diff for D1 set rows, D2 Why? sheet, D3 wizard copy, D6 AI consent sheet                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | lanes touching them | ✔                   |
| 8     | Wave 4 only: VoiceOver + TalkBack on hardware (V9), proxy check of analytics (T-12 AC3), counsel checklist, **native release smoke** (both production builds installed via `pnpm mobile:release:*`, then an OTA published and received — the More footer shows the update id; Android export arrives as a named file)                                                                                                                                                                | —                   | wave 4              |

Never mark a level passed that could not be run (mobile_native_plan.md §4 rule) — report the gap in the PR.

### 7.6 Definition of done (every task)

1. Code follows C1–C10 (layering, Zod, shared-first, additive API, no `process.env` outside `env.ts`, no new hex
   literals or inline user strings in touched mobile components — P12).
2. The task's acceptance criteria (from its UX spec) pass, with evidence in the PR.
3. Tests at every applicable ladder level exist and pass; bug tasks have a regression test named after the bug id.
4. Web parity shipped in the same PR group, or a `mobile_parity_backlog.md` row (reverse rows for gym/landing).
5. Docs updated per §8 in the same PR.
6. Analytics events added through the typed `EventMap` with enum/count properties only.
7. Flags: new behaviour behind its flag where §2.9 says so, default off; the flag is listed in `infrastructure.md` §10.
8. OTA-safety: fingerprint unchanged (or a native release scheduled and the owner told); the API the JS needs is
   deployed first (wave merge order guarantees it).
9. Installed-binary compatibility run green for API changes.
10. Protected delights untouched (empty diffs) unless the spec says otherwise.

**Added**

  - T-21.1 one local-day contract (B-06, B-33).
- Next: T-17.6 (B-51), UX-23 (moved from Later), UX-37. Later: UX-38.
- Bug tasks T-BUG-33…53.
- §2.11 targets resolver + change detection, §2.12 local-day contract, §2.13 consent log / emails / export,
  §2.14 native-release decision.
- Schema S11–S17. New routers `targets`, `training`, `privacy`.
- A new lane **L-TRACK**. Questions Q-15–Q-21.

**Changed**

- Tasks: T-05.3 is now the readable routine editor (settings and reminders moved to T-36.1/36.2). T-04.2 lost the
  week-matching (now T-00.15). T-08.4 lost the B-13 data fix. T-07.1's API is renamed `mealPlan.getShape/setShape`.
  Health-consent procedures moved to the `privacy` router. T-26.4 now covers only the health rows; the privacy
  section is T-39.4. T-21.5 moved its tracker pieces to L-TRACK. Rev-1 T-BUG-23 is now T-19.4.
- Analytics and consent: T-12 adds the privacy-by-default constraints and logs every switch change. The health
  consent's source of truth is the `ConsentEvent` log (`healthDataWithdrawnAt` dropped).
- Waves and ownership: rebuilt to match the UX packaging (§7.1 lists the deviations). Q-15 of rev 1 is resolved by
  stage 1 (it is bug B-13).
- **Renumbered:** rev 1's source-found `T-BUG-40…45` became `T-BUG-X1…X6` (collision with stage-1 B-40…B-45), and
  X7 was added. This is the only ID change; no `T-xx.n` was renumbered.

**Native-release discrepancy with stage 3 (resolved here, noted in both documents)**

- Stage 3 §7 plans one wave-4 native release for (a) the PostHog RN SDK (UX-12) and (b) `expo-file-system` +
  `expo-sharing` (UX-39).
- This plan keeps **one** wave-4 native release, but it contains only `expo-sharing`, plus `expo-file-system`
  pinned as a direct dependency:
  - UX-12 uses the pure-JS transport (T-12.2), so it needs no native module.
  - `ExpoFileSystem` 57.0.6 is already linked in the installed binaries through `expo`.
  - The Android file-share path (`expo-sharing`) is the only missing native capability.

  Details in §2.14. Stage 3 Appendix B carries the matching note.

| **W4 native release** (adds)            | T-BUG-O1.2 (client photo resize), T-05.A1.3 (gesture-handler swipe, only if the JS swipe fails QA)                                                                | 2     | S     | Native       |

(T-BUG-O4, the cardio bug, is closed by tasks already counted: T-05.A2.1 in W1 stops the nonsense coaching,
T-42.x models cardio.)

**Schema (all additive, `db push`-safe):** S18–S21 cardio (`Exercise.trackingType` with a default, 10 cardio
`ExerciseEquipment` values, 7 nullable `SessionSet` columns, `GymProfile.distanceUnit?`) in **W2-0** if D-20 = b,
else W5-0; S22–S28 in **W5-0**: routine cardio targets, `UserRole.TRAINER`, exercise provenance
(`origin?`, `authorId?`), **NEW** `ExerciseRequest`, **NEW** `ExerciseNameMapping`, **NEW** id-keyed `Ingredient`
catalogue and `IngredientImportRun`. W0-D, W1 and UX-44 need **no** schema change: a past-session delete rides the
existing idempotent upsert as `status: DISCARDED` (Δ2.3), and `pausedAt` is device-only.

**Native rebuild impact.** Everything in this delta ships over OTA **except** client-side photo resizing
(`expo-image-manipulator`, W4). One correction to the plan's facts table (§1): **`react-native-gesture-handler`
3.2.1 is already linked in both release binaries** (`ios/Podfile.lock` `RNGestureHandler (3.2.1)`; the APK carries
`libgesturehandler.so`), pulled in transitively by `expo-router` → `react-native-drawer-layout`. It is not a direct
dependency of `apps/mobile`, so JS cannot import it reliably under pnpm. PAT-16's swipe therefore ships JS-first
(`PanResponder` + Reanimated, as 03 designed); if that fails QA, making RNGH a direct dependency **at exactly 3.2.1** may
keep the runtime fingerprint (ladder 3f decides), and only if the fingerprint moves does the swipe wait for W4 (Q-29).

**Top risks.** (1) **Installed binaries and cardio:** old bundles must never receive a cardio exercise or cardio
set they would render as `0 kg × 0`; delivery is gated by `x-chefer-api-level` (Δ2.1). (2) **Role enforcement** on
`createCustom` breaks the old binary's `+ Custom`; it is a flag first, a readable `FORBIDDEN` later (Δ2.4). (3)
**W1 L-GYM load:** it was already the largest lane and gains 9 tasks; the cut order is in Δ7.4. (4) **Ingredient
licence** (D-16): no import before counsel. (5) **Engine parity:** UX-05 A2 changes starting suggestions for
timed/custom exercises on phone and server; it rides T-05.1's `ENGINE_VERSION` 1 → 2 bump in the same wave.

<a id="d1-root-causes"></a>

### Δ1 Owner-reported bugs: root causes (from the source at `8c9de2c`)

#### T-BUG-O1 — Recipe photo upload shows `[object Object]` and the photo is not added (O-18)

**Two defects, one certain, one very likely.**

1. **Certain: the client stringifies an error object.** `apps/mobile/src/lib/media-client.ts` L86–88:
   `new Error(data?.error ?? …)` types `error` as a string, but every error that reaches Express's global handler is
   answered as `{ success: false, error: { code, message } }` (`apps/api/src/index.ts` L201–211; the 404 handler
   L191–196 has the same shape). `new Error({…})` has the message `"[object Object]"`, which
   `app/recipe-form.tsx` L116–117 renders under the button. `scanMealPhoto` (L60–70) has the same bug, so Snap-to-log
   inherits it; web's `apps/web/src/lib/upload-image.ts` L24–25 and `features/tracker/lib/scan-client.ts` L40–50 read
   the same field.
2. **Very likely: the photo is larger than 5 MB, and the 413 becomes a 500.** `apps/api/src/routers/uploads.router.ts`
   L16 and L38 parse the body with `express.raw({ type: 'image/*', limit: 5 MB })`. An oversize body makes body-parser
   throw `PayloadTooLargeError` (`status 413`, `type 'entity.too.large'`) **before** the handler runs; the router has
   no error middleware, so the global handler answers **500** with the object shape. The mobile picker asks for
   `quality: 0.8` + `base64: true` with no size check (`app/recipe-form.tsx` L98–102), and an iPhone 17 camera photo
   re-encoded at 0.8 is typically 5–10 MB. Web never hits this because it checks 5 MB before sending
   (`upload-image.ts` L12). The only other path to a 500 with that shape is a disk write failure (L72–73). **Confirm in
   the production logs** before merging: `unhandled express error` with `err.type = 'entity.too.large'` (or an `EACCES`
   / `ENOSPC` on `writeFile`).
3. Also noted: `recipe-form.tsx` L109 labels every non-PNG asset `image/jpeg`; a HEIC that was not transcoded would
   be answered with a readable 415 (sniff mismatch), not `[object Object]`, so it is not this bug, but the fix logs
   the MIME type and size bucket on failure.

**Fix:** T-BUG-O1 in W0-D (Δ5.0); the root cause (resize before upload) is T-BUG-O1.2 in W4.

#### T-BUG-O2 — Set 2's kg and reps render at ~4 pt (O-07)

`packages/ui-mobile/src/components/value-stepper.tsx` L110–118: the value is
`<Text numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>` with **no `minimumFontScale`**, so there is no floor. It sits in a
`PressableScale` (a Reanimated animated `Pressable`, L102–108) that is `min-w-9 flex-1`, and the set row
(`apps/mobile/src/features/gym/workout/set-row.tsx` L147–179) packs two such steppers plus a 56 pt ✓ with `gap-1`: on a
375 pt phone each value cell is about 38 pt wide. The shrink factor is computed against the cell's width **at measure
time** and, on the new architecture, it is not recomputed upwards when the cell later widens. Any transient narrow
measure of one row (a re-layout when a propagated weight or a tick changes that row, while its siblings don't
re-measure) leaves that one row's values at a tiny scale. That matches the screenshot exactly: one row tiny, identical
neighbours normal. The transient cannot be proven without a device trace, so the fix **removes the mechanism**:
no `adjustsFontSizeToFit`; the font size is a pure function of the string. The same prop exists at
`app/(food)/meal-plan.tsx` L324 (3-letter day labels, bounded by `maxFontSizeMultiplier`); it is lower risk and left
alone. The summary screen's AdjustSheet (`workout/summary-screen.tsx` L430–453) uses the same `ValueStepper` and
gets the fix for free.

#### T-BUG-O3 — "Edit created recipe is not working properly" (O-15)

There is no screenshot, so this is a **candidate list with a repro plan**. Every candidate is a real defect in
`apps/mobile/app/recipe-form.tsx`; the first two are the likeliest explanation of what the owner saw.

| #   | Candidate (certain as a code defect unless marked)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Where                | Symptom the owner would see                  |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| C1  | **Stale prefill reverts earlier edits.** The form prefills once (`prefilled` guard, L123–155) from `recipe.getMyRecipe` (L71–74), and a successful save invalidates only `recipe.list` (L157–162). Reopening Edit while the query is still cached (react-query's default `gcTime` 5 min; food queries are memory-only) prefills from the **pre-edit** copy and ignores the refetch; saving again reverts the first edit. Web has the same pattern (`app/(dashboard)/recipes/[id]/edit/page.tsx` L80, `hydrated` guard L106–116, no invalidation at L151–152). | L71–74, L123–162     | "My change didn't stick" / "it went back"    |
| C2  | **Diet tags wiped** (`dietaryTags: []`, L194) — bug B-01, T-01.6                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | L194                 | a vegetarian recipe becomes "non-vegetarian" |
| C3  | **Lines silently dropped or changed.** `num()` (L61–64) uses `parseFloat`: `½` → 0, so the line is filtered out on save (L165–167); `1/2` → 1                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | L61–64, L165–167     | an ingredient disappears or doubles          |
| C4  | **Save looks dead.** `canSave` needs description, cuisine and ≥ 1 step (L169–175) with no reason shown (O-14); the `ScrollView` (L231) has no `keyboardShouldPersistTaps`, so with the keyboard up the first tap on `Save changes` only dismisses the keyboard                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | L169–175, L231, L433 | "Save does nothing"                          |
| C5  | **Load error renders an empty form.** An error from `getMyRecipe` (deleted recipe, stale list) is not handled (L207–213): the screen shows a blank `Edit Recipe` form with a disabled button                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | L71–74, L207–213     | an empty edit form                           |
| C6  | **Fiber overwritten with 0** on every mobile edit (L191); a recipe created on web loses its fiber                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | L186–192             | invisible on mobile; wrong on web            |
| C7  | **Raw server message** on a save error (L427–431), e.g. a Zod issue list                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | L427–431             | an unreadable error                          |

**Repro plan (first step of T-BUG-O3, on the owner's iPhone build and the Pixel 8 emulator, against the local API):**
(1) create a recipe with a vegetarian tag, `½ tsp salt`, a photo and 3 steps on **web**; (2) on mobile, edit one
ingredient and save; reopen Edit at once: check the fields show the new value (C1), the tags (C2), the salt line (C3),
the photo; (3) save again without changes, then read the recipe on web: tags, fiber (C6), lines; (4) with the keyboard
up, tap `Save changes` once (C4); (5) delete the recipe on web, then open Edit from the stale mobile list (C5); (6) send
an over-long name through the API to see C7. Ask the owner which of these matches (05 §5 follow-up). **Fix all seven
regardless**; they are each ≤ S and all fall inside UX-40 slice 1's rebuild of the same file.

#### T-BUG-O4 — Cardio is forced into sets × kg (O-01, O-02, O-03)

There is exactly one logging shape. `schema.prisma` L855–893 (`Exercise`: `isTimed` only, no tracking type),
L1036–1049 (`SessionSet`: `weightKg` × `reps` only) and L808–819 (`ExerciseEquipment`: no cardio machine). The owner's
"Indoor bike" is a custom exercise; the form (`library-screens/exercise-form-screen.tsx` L71–81 defaults
`BARBELL · WEIGHTED · 8–12`, L346–349 the `Timed exercise` checkbox) cannot express time + distance, so he picked a
machine, ticked timed and set the range to 1. Then, in order:

- `workout/workout-model.ts` L334–342 `defaultSlotParams()` → `sets: 3` for any exercise (the routine editor's
  `routine/reducer.ts` L60–68 `newExerciseRow` does the same) → `3 × … · 0/3 done`;
- L301–305 `weightModeOf()` ignores `isTimed` → a kg stepper on every row;
- L371–402 `prescribeFor()` → `packages/utils/src/gym/progression.ts` L202–233 `initialState()` →
  `startingGuessKg()` L132–177: a MACHINE stack is `machineStepKg` (5) × 3 plates for a beginner compound = **15 kg**
  (calibration is skipped for timed exercises, L107–109);
- `packages/utils/src/gym/reasons.ts` L86–89: `START` on a `WEIGHTED` load type → **`Starting weight: 15 kg. Aim for
1 s.`** (`aimText` L59, with `repMin = 1`);
- `packages/utils/src/gym/duration.ts` L14–19 estimates every exercise as `sets × (40 s + rest)`.

**Fix:** W1 T-05.A2.1 stops the nonsense (no load, sane aim, neutral first-time sentence); W2 T-42.0–T-42.5 (D-20 b)
or W5 adds the cardio shape (06 §5); the custom form's `How do you track it?` (T-42.3) makes the bike impossible to
create in the strength shape again.

#### T-BUG-X8 — Private ingredient names are global keys (source-found)

`IngredientPrice.ingredientName` is the primary key (`schema.prisma` L755–778), shared by global rows and every
user's private rows. `ingredients.createCustom` (`application/ingredients/ingredients.service.ts` L286–333) therefore
answers another user's attempt with `CONFLICT: Ingredient name is already taken.`, which **reveals that some other
account owns a private ingredient of that name** (low severity, but it breaks UX-41 AC2, "visible only to its
creator"). It also means a private row can never coexist with a global row of the same name (UX-41's "both appear,
Yours first") and a licensed import would collide with private rows. Fixed structurally by the id-keyed `Ingredient`
table in W5 (Δ2.5); the message becomes generic in the same task.

<a id="d2-design"></a>

### Δ2 Cross-cutting design

#### Δ2.1 Client API levels 2 and 3 (extends §2.8)

`ctx.clientApiLevel` exists since W0 (`apps/api/src/lib/trpc.ts` L20–26; clients send level 1). This delta gives the
header two more meanings. A level is only sent by a bundle that implements it; the bump is a one-line change in
`apps/mobile/src/lib/trpc-links.ts` and `apps/web/src/lib/{trpc-provider.tsx,trpc-server.ts}`, made **in the same PR
as the UI that honours it**.

| Level | Sent by                            | Means the client…                                                                                                                                             |
| ----- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 0     | installed binaries before W0's OTA | knows nothing below                                                                                                                                           |
| 1     | W0+                                | handles the health-consent error (§2.8)                                                                                                                       |
| **2** | W2 L-GYM's OTA (D-20 b) or W5      | renders `trackingType` `DURATION`, `DURATION_DISTANCE`, `DISTANCE`, the cardio `SessionSet` fields and the cardio equipment values; logs cardio as one entry  |
| **3** | W5 L-GYMDATA's OTA                 | also renders `INTERVALS`, requested exercises (`origin: REQUESTED`, status chips) and routine cardio slots; calls `requestExercise` instead of `createCustom` |

**Server rule (one helper, NEW `apps/api/src/application/gym/client-level.ts`):** `renderableTrackingTypes(level)`
= strength types + `DURATION` at levels 0–1 (a timed exercise already renders), + `DURATION_DISTANCE`, `DISTANCE` at
2, + `INTERVALS` at 3. It is applied in exactly four read paths: `gym.bootstrap` (`library`, `recentSessions`,
`nextWorkout`), `gym.library.list`, `gym.session.get`, `gym.session.list`. For a low level they **drop** library rows
of a non-renderable type and **drop session exercises** of that type from session payloads (the session itself stays,
so week counts and streaks are unchanged). A routine returned to a level < 3 client has its cardio slots removed, and
`gym.routine.save` from a level < 3 client **re-inserts** the stored cardio slots at their positions (routine saves
are full-replace; without this, an old client's save would silently delete them). Contract tests assert all four
paths at levels 0, 1, 2 and 3.

**When a client moves up a level** (it takes the OTA), its persisted library cache was filtered, and `librarySince`
would never re-send the rows it missed. The OTA that bumps the level also bumps `GYM_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION`
(`src/features/gym/offline/query-persistence.ts` L13) so the persisted cache is dropped and the next bootstrap is a
full one.

#### Δ2.2 Cardio data model (06 §5, additive) and what old clients see

- `Exercise.trackingType ExerciseTrackingType @default(WEIGHT_REPS)` (enum `WEIGHT_REPS · BODYWEIGHT_REPS · DURATION ·
DURATION_DISTANCE · DISTANCE · INTERVALS`; all six land at once, `INTERVALS` unused until W5). The code catalogue
  sync writes it for curated rows; an idempotent boot backfill sets `DURATION` on custom rows with `isTimed = true`
  and `BODYWEIGHT_REPS` on `loadType = BODYWEIGHT` rows (the `backfillLegacyServingSizes` pattern). `isTimed` stays and
  is still written (`true` for every time-based type) so a level-0/1 client keeps its seconds label.
- `SessionSet` + `durationSec Int?`, `distanceM Float?`, `intensityRpe Int?` (1–10), `resistanceLevel Int?`,
  `inclinePct Float?`, `caloriesKcal Float?`, `avgHeartRateBpm Int?` (a reserved slot, never written from manual
  entry). A cardio entry is **one** `SessionSet` row with `weightKg: 0`, `reps: 0`, `isWarmup: false`; `INTERVALS`
  (W5) is one row per work or rest segment, `position` = segment index (06 §5.2).
- **Session doc (wire):** `sessionSetDocSchema` gains the same seven fields as `.optional()`. `schemaVersion` stays
  `1` (the change is additive; an old server strips unknown keys, and the API deploys first anyway).
- **Prescription for a cardio slot:** `suggestionSchema` embeds `reasonCode: z.enum(REASON_CODES)`, and installed
  apps park docs that fail it (§1 facts). So cardio uses **no new `ReasonCode`**: `kind: 'hold'`, `weightKg: 0`,
  `reps: []`, `sets: 0`, `reasonCode: 'START'` on first use, then `'CONSOLIDATE'` (hold) or `'ADD_REPS'` (progress; W5),
  all existing codes, with every cardio fact in `inputs` (`trackingType`, `targetDurationSec`, `targetDistanceM`, `targetRpe`, `lastDurationSec`, …).
  The cardio sentence is rendered by NEW `packages/utils/src/gym/cardio.ts` from those inputs, never by `explain()`.
- **Progression:** cardio has **no stored progression state**. `progression.recompute` skips non-strength
  tracking types; the cardio "Next time" is a pure function of the last entries in `bootstrap.recentSessions`
  (12 weeks, cached offline). PR, e1RM and volume code (`prs.ts`, `e1rm.ts`, `volume.ts`, `gym-stats.service.ts`)
  skip cardio exercises.
- **Units:** `GymProfile.distanceUnit DistanceUnit?` (`KM | MI`); null ⇒ `MI` when `unit = LB`, else `KM`. Stored
  canonically in metres.
- **Old clients (levels 0–1):** they never receive a cardio library row, a cardio session exercise or a routine
  cardio slot (Δ2.1). History on an old binary shows a mixed session's strength part, and a cardio-only session as a
  named session with no exercises; week counts are unaffected. If a cardio set ever did reach an old bundle, it would
  render `0 kg × 0` from the placeholders, never crash (no schema change there, unknown keys ignored). This is
  accepted as Q-34.

#### Δ2.3 Correcting a past session through the existing outbox (UX-44; no API change)

`gym.session.upsertMany` already (a) replaces children in a transaction, (b) guards with last-write-wins on
`clientUpdatedAt`, (c) recomputes progression for the exercises of the new **and** the previous version of a
`COMPLETED` doc (`application/gym/workout-session.service.ts` L141–181), and (d) accepts `status: DISCARDED`, which
every list, bootstrap and stat excludes (`workout-session.repository.ts` L95, L235). Therefore:

- **Edit** = load the stored doc (bootstrap cache or `gym.session.get`) into an edit draft, change it, bump
  `clientUpdatedAt`, enqueue it in the existing outbox. Offline-safe, idempotent, recomputed server-side.
- **Delete with Undo** = hold the delete on the device for the 8 s snackbar, then enqueue the same doc with
  `status: 'DISCARDED'` and a bumped `clientUpdatedAt`. It works offline and needs **no new outbox op and no schema**.
  When the upsert is acknowledged and the device is online, the client also calls the existing
  `gym.session.delete` (hard delete, recomputes) so no soft-deleted row lingers (Q-30). The old detail-screen path
  (`history/session-detail-screen.tsx` L35–56, native `Alert`, online-only) goes.
- **The target-change notice (PAT-14)** is derived on the client: snapshot `bootstrap.progressions[].state.next` for
  the touched exercises before enqueueing; after the next bootstrap refetch, diff. `Keep the old ones` calls the
  existing `gym.progression.setOverride` (`routers/gym/progression.router.ts` L11–13). No server change.

#### Δ2.4 A role restriction on custom exercises that old binaries survive (UX-43)

- **Role:** `UserRole` + `TRAINER` (Prisma enum and `packages/types/src/index.ts` L22–27, the constant object the
  routers' `z.nativeEnum(UserRole)` reads). `isPremiumUser()` (`apps/api/src/lib/entitlements.ts`) and mobile's
  `use-is-premium.ts` L13 compare to `ADMIN` only, so **a trainer gets no premium** (D-11; UX-43 AC9 is a test).
- **Middleware (CLAUDE.md rule 5):** NEW `trainerProcedure` in `apps/api/src/lib/trpc.ts` next to `adminProcedure`
  (L139–168): allows `TRAINER | ADMIN`. Library authoring and request resolution use it. `gym.library.createCustom`
  moves onto NEW `customAuthoringProcedure` = `protectedProcedure.use(requireAuthoringRole)`, which rejects a `USER`
  only when `EXERCISE_AUTHORING_ENFORCE=on` (NEW env, default `off`, validated in `env.ts`), with `FORBIDDEN` and the
  message `Adding exercises is now done by Chefer’s trainers. Update the app to request one.`
- **What old binaries do:** before enforcement, nothing changes. After, an old binary's `+ Custom` form submits and
  shows the sentence in its existing error list (`exercise-form-screen.tsx` L110–116 renders `err.message`). Its
  existing customs stay listed, usable and editable (`updateCustom` / `archiveCustom` stay owner-only
  `protectedProcedure`, D-15). **Rollout** (same as `HEALTH_CONSENT_ENFORCE`): ship API + OTA with the
  `exerciseRequests` flag off → turn the flag on once UX-42's catalogue and the gap pass are live (UX-43 AC7) → flip
  `EXERCISE_AUTHORING_ENFORCE=on` only after both owner devices show the new OTA id in More's footer (Q-10's check).
- **The boot sync would archive trainer rows.** `syncExerciseLibrary()` (`apps/api/src/lib/exercise-library/ensure.ts`
  L103–138) archives every curated (`ownerId = null`) row whose id is not in the code catalogue. Trainer-authored
  library rows therefore need provenance: `Exercise.origin ExerciseOrigin?` (null ⇒ derived: `ownerId` null →
  `CATALOG`, else `CUSTOM`) and `findAllCurated()` must return `origin ∈ {null, CATALOG}` only. A test pins this.

#### Δ2.5 The ingredient catalogue needs an id-keyed table (UX-41)

`IngredientPrice` is keyed by `ingredientName` (normalised name), shared by global and private rows (T-BUG-X8). UX-41
needs a private row next to a same-name catalogue row, merges that move links, renames that keep links, and a
licensed import that cannot collide with private rows. Under `db push` the primary key cannot change, so W5-0 adds
**NEW `Ingredient`** (cuid id; `ownerKey` = `creatorId ?? 'global'` with `@@unique([normalizedName, ownerKey])`,
because Postgres treats nulls as distinct in a composite unique). Transition, all idempotent code:

1. **Boot backfill** copies every `IngredientPrice` row into `Ingredient` (global rows `source: CHEFER_ESTIMATE`,
   private rows `USER`), keeping `priceKey = ingredientName`.
2. **Reads switch** to `Ingredient` in L-INGR: `ingredients.search`, `list`, `computeNutrition`, and T-19.1's tracker
   search (the tracker service calls the ingredients repository, so one change covers it). Output shapes are
   unchanged, with additive fields (`id`, `source`, `verified`, `allergens`).
3. **Writes** (`createCustom`, `update`, `delete`, admin verify/merge) go to `Ingredient`. `IngredientPrice` stays the
   **price** vocabulary: the AI worker (`workers/ingredient-price.worker.ts`) keeps estimating prices by name, and new
   AI-estimated names are mirrored into `Ingredient` as `CHEFER_ESTIMATE` rows when missing. **Verified rows are never
   re-estimated** (the worker's `source: { not: 'AI_ESTIMATE' }` rule at L140–149 already protects them; their
   macros come from the licensed source, only prices are refreshed).
4. **Recipe lines** keep `{ name, quantity, unit }` and gain an optional `ingredientId` inside the JSON (no DDL).

A cheaper option (extend `IngredientPrice` with columns and live with name keys) is recorded as Q-25; it drops
UX-41's same-name edge case and makes merges rename rows.

#### Δ2.6 Swipe-to-remove without a native release (PAT-16)

NEW `apps/mobile/src/components/swipe-to-remove.tsx`: RN core `PanResponder` driving a Reanimated shared value (both in
the binary). It claims the gesture only when `|dx| > 12` and `|dx| > 2 × |dy|`; it also exposes
`accessibilityActions` and is always paired with a visible `⋯` menu path, so the menu satisfies every acceptance
criterion on its own. QA on both OSes inside the workout `ScrollView`; if the responder fights the scroll, ship the
menu + Undo and take T-05.A1.3 (Q-29).

#### Δ2.7 How catalogue additions and re-cropped photos reach installed apps (UX-05 A5, A6)

- **New exercises need no migration and no seed step.** `ensureExerciseLibrary()` runs at API boot
  (`apps/api/src/index.ts` L250) and creates any catalogue row that is missing (`ensure.ts` L110–117) with a fresh
  `updatedAt`. Every client, including installed binaries, receives new rows through `gym.bootstrap`'s
  `librarySince` filter (`gym-bootstrap.service.ts` L109–110) on its next bootstrap. The photos must be in the same
  deploy: `imageKeysFor()` (`ensure.ts` L45–50) only lists files that exist in `apps/api/static/exercises/` at sync
  time, so a row synced before its photos stays photo-less until the next content change. **Post-deploy check:** the
  API log line `[exercise-library] ensured N curated exercises (created 2, …)`.
- **Re-cropped photos need new file names.** Files are served under fixed names (`<slug>-0.webp`, static `maxAge`
  7 d) and expo-image caches by URL, so re-vendoring in place would leave old crops on devices, and an unchanged
  `imageKeys` does not bump `updatedAt`, so incremental clients would not even be told. T-05.11 writes the
  normalised 600 × 400 files as **`<slug>-0.3x2.webp` / `<slug>-1.3x2.webp`**, `imageKeysFor()` prefers the new names
  (falls back to the old ones), the old files are removed in the same PR. The changed `imageKeys` bump
  `contentVersion` and `updatedAt` (`ensure.ts` L118–127), so every client, old binaries included, gets the new URLs
  on its next bootstrap.

#### Δ2.8 Flags and env added (extends §2.9)

| Key                          | Kind                        | Default                                 | Gates                                                                               | Task           |
| ---------------------------- | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------- |
| `cardioLogging`              | flag (`profile.flags`)      | off; on after the W2 owner check (Q-24) | the `Cardio` chip, cardio entry, the custom form's time-based choices (client side) | T-42.3         |
| `cardioCaloriesOnSummary`    | flag                        | off (⚖ D-17 a)                          | the calorie range on the workout summary                                            | T-42.10        |
| `exerciseRequests`           | flag                        | off until T-43.1 is live (UX-43 AC7)    | `Request an exercise` replacing `+ Custom`                                          | T-43.2, T-43.7 |
| `EXERCISE_AUTHORING_ENFORCE` | env (`off \| on`), `env.ts` | `off`                                   | `customAuthoringProcedure` rejecting `USER` on `createCustom`                       | T-43.7         |

Flags are typed keys added to `packages/types/src/feature-flags.ts`; the env var goes to `infrastructure.md` §10 and
`apps/api/.env.example` in W5-0.

updates `infrastructure.md` §6. **No lane edits `schema.prisma`** except the contracts step of its wave (W2-0, W5-0).

"Keep mine" is remembered in `GymProfile.offerState` (existing JSON).                                                                                     |

**Deliberately not schema:** the recipe line's `ingredientId` and `nutritionInfo.source` (`none | manual | computed`)
are additive keys inside existing JSON columns (`Recipe.ingredients`, `Recipe.nutritionInfo`); the Resume card's
`pausedAt` and the cardio timer (`{ seId, startedAt, accumulatedSec }`) live next to the doc in the on-device
active-session store and are never uploaded; a past-session delete is a `DISCARDED` upsert (Δ2.3); `Keep mine` and
the relink dismissals use `GymProfile.offerState`.

Client: NEW pure `uploadErrorFrom(status, body, bytes)` in `media-client.ts` reads `error` as a string **or** `{ code, message }` and maps to UX-40's four sentences (413 or over the limit → `That photo is too big. <a id="d6-data"></a>

### Δ6 Data work

#### Δ6.1 Seeding the ingredient catalogue (⚖ D-16)

| Option (05 §5 D-16)                                    | Licence (my understanding; **counsel confirms before any import**, B-26) | Use                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| (a) USDA FoodData Central: Foundation Foods, SR Legacy | Public domain (CC0); attribution requested, not required                 | **Recommended primary**: the widest generic coverage, nutrient ids are stable (energy 1008, protein 1003, fat 1004, carbohydrate 1005, fiber 1079), and `foodPortion` rows give piece and cup weights |
| (b) ANSES CIQUAL                                       | Licence Ouverte / Etalab 2.0: reuse allowed, **attribution required**    | **Recommended secondary** for European generics the FDC lacks or describes differently                                                                                                                |
| (c) UK CoFID (McCance & Widdowson)                     | Terms to check                                                           | Not planned                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| (d) Open Food Facts                                    | ODbL (share-alike on the database) and branded                           | **Rejected** (B-29 boundary and licence)                                                                                                                                                              |
| (e) AI estimates + admin review                        | —                                                                        | **Build default until D-16 and counsel answer**: every row reads `Estimated`; the review queue works on them                                                                                          |

**Recommended default for the build:** build the pipeline for (a) + (b), ship (e) until the owner answers D-16 and
counsel confirms the attribution wording; then flip by committing the seed file (a data PR, no code change).

**Pipeline (T-41.1).**

1. **Selection** (`packages/database/seed-data/ingredients/selection.csv`, committed): one row per generic
   ingredient: `canonicalName, source, sourceRef (FDC id or CIQUAL code), naturalUnit, gramsPerPiece?,
densityGPerMl?, synonyms_ro, allergens?, diets?`. Seeded from three lists, deduplicated by normalised name: the
   distinct ingredient names in the database (the worker's `collectVocabulary()`), the curated recipe pool, and a
   common-staples list; target **800–1,500 rows** (05 §2.1). A helper proposes FDC/CIQUAL matches; a person confirms
   each row (the CSV is the reviewed artefact).
2. **Build** (`scripts/ingredients/build-catalogue.ts`, offline, reads the downloaded FDC/CIQUAL files, which are
   **not** committed): per-100 g macros from the source, piece and volume conversions from portions,
   `catalogue.v1.json` + `LICENSES.md` (source, version, date, attribution text).
3. **Sync** (`ensureIngredientCatalog()` at API boot): upsert by `(source, sourceRef)`; a row an admin has edited
   after the import is not overwritten (only reported); a name that exists as an AI estimate is upgraded in place
   (same id, so links survive); private rows are never touched; the run writes `IngredientImportRun`.
4. **Allergens and diets** are not in FDC or CIQUAL. The build proposes EU-14 tags with the safety taxonomy's pattern
   lists (`packages/types/src/safety-taxonomy.ts`, `lib/curated-recipes/safety.ts`); they are **unverified** until an
   admin marks the row verified in the review queue. An unreviewed row with no tags never shows "No EU-listed
   allergens" (the detail sheet shows `Estimated by Chefer · not yet checked`).
5. **Romanian names** are synonyms added by admins in the review queue (B-18/B-24), never machine-translated into
   `name`.

#### Δ6.2 Cardio catalogue seed (06 §6)

- **W2 (D-20 b), 12 entries**, `DURATION` / `DURATION_DISTANCE` only: `treadmill-walk`, `treadmill-incline-walk`,
  `treadmill-run`, `outdoor-walk`, `outdoor-run`, `outdoor-cycle`, `stationary-bike-upright`,
  `stationary-bike-recumbent`, `spin-class` (`DURATION`), `elliptical`, `rowing-machine`, `stair-climber` (`DURATION`).
- **W5, the other 18** incl. the 7 `INTERVALS` rows, ski erg, assault bike, jump rope, swimming, sled work, battle
  ropes, boxing, loaded carries, stadium stairs, the weighted ruck.
- **Row shape:** the catalogue's structural fields get neutral values (`category: COMPOUND`, `movementPattern:
'cardio-<kind>'`, `repMin = repMax = 1`, `restSec: 0`, `incrementKg: 0`, `isTimed: true`, `loadType: BODYWEIGHT`)
  so every existing reader stays valid; `trackingType` and the cardio equipment carry the meaning. Presets
  (`10 min · easy`, `20 min · moderate`, `30 min · easy`), default metrics and the MET low/high live in
  `cardio-catalog.ts`. **MET values are checked against pacompendium.com per activity before merge** (06 §6's note).
  Cues come from 06's table; photos are the T-05.11 placeholder (equipment icon) until D-22 (b) art.
- **Delivery:** the boot sync (Δ2.7), filtered by client level (Δ2.1).

this delta adds. Each has the default this plan builds.

| ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |

Worst-case disk per user stays capped at 60 uploads a day                                                             | Yes                                                                                             | T-BUG-O1             |

  (unbounded `adjustsFontSizeToFit` on a flexible 38 pt cell), T-BUG-O3 (seven candidates, the likeliest a stale

  T-BUG-O1.2, T-05.A1.3; W5 T-41.0–T-41.6, T-42.0b, T-42.6–T-42.10, T-43.0–T-43.8, T-01.A1.1, T-11.A1.1, T-19.A1.1,
